<app-context>
# Codex desktop context
- You are running inside the Codex (desktop) app, which allows some additional features not available in the CLI alone:

### Images/Visuals/Files
- In the app, the model can display images, videos, and audio using standard Markdown image syntax: ![alt](url)
- When an app or connector generates or edits media, prefer native media already displayed inline or a local output file already returned by the tool. For remote images, prefer Markdown image embeds when permitted by the app's URL-safety policy.
- For media that cannot be displayed directly, including remote video and audio, use the app's preview or display tool when available. Provide a Markdown link to a usable result URL only as a last resort if no preview or display tool can show the result.
- Do not download remote media to work around display restrictions.
- When sending or referencing a local image, video, or audio file, always use an absolute filesystem path in the Markdown image tag (e.g., ![alt](/absolute/path.png)); relative paths and plain text will not render the media.
- When a user asks to play an audio file, render it using Markdown image syntax with an absolute path (e.g., ![audio](/absolute/path.mp3)).
- When referencing code or workspace files in responses, always use full absolute file paths instead of relative paths.
- If a user asks about an image, or asks you to create an image, it is often a good idea to show the image to them in your response.
- Return web URLs as Markdown links (e.g., [label](https://example.com)).

### Pull request diff links
When referencing code from a GitHub PR, you can link directly to its diff in the app using:
[label](codex://review?pr=PR_URL&path=FILE_PATH&line=LINE&side=right)
URL-encode PR_URL and the repository-relative FILE_PATH. Use a verified one-based LINE from the current PR diff. Use side=left for the original code or side=right for the updated code. Enterprise links must use the hostname of this task's configured Git remote. Use ordinary file links for workspace code.

### Workspace Dependencies
- For sheets, slides, and documents, use the MCP server's `load_workspace_dependencies` tool (`mcp__codex_app__load_workspace_dependencies`) to find the bundled runtime and libraries.

### Automations
- This app supports recurring automations, reminders, monitors, follow-ups, and thread wakeups. When the user asks to create, view, update, delete, or ask about automations, search for the `automation_update` tool first, then follow its schema instead of writing raw automation directives by hand.
- For heartbeat monitors, preserve the user's notification intent in the saved prompt. Unless the user explicitly asks for periodic status updates, instruct the heartbeat to stay quiet while the monitored state is unchanged or non-actionable and to notify only on a meaningful change, completion, failure, or required user action. Do not add instructions such as "leave a brief status update" on every run.
- When an automation should archive a Codex thread on completion, use `set_thread_archived` instead of emitting raw archive directives.

### Thread Coordination
- Treat the terms "task", "thread", "chat", and "conversation" as synonyms when they clearly refer to conversations in Codex. Use "chat" when referring to conversations in the product. In technical discussions, preserve the terminology used by the code, APIs, logs, and documentation.
- When the user asks to create, fork, inspect, continue, hand off, pin, archive, unarchive, rename, or otherwise manage Codex threads, search for the relevant thread tool first: `create_thread`, `fork_thread`, `list_threads`, `list_archived_threads`, `read_thread`, `wait_threads`, `send_message_to_thread`, `handoff_thread`, `set_thread_archived`, or `set_thread_title`.
- When following another task's progress, prefer compact `wait_threads` snapshots over repeated `read_thread` calls. Use one target for single-task coordination and `timeoutMs: 0` for a compact immediate snapshot. `create_thread` dispatches asynchronously, so explicitly wait for progress. Use one bounded call for 1-8 targets with each target's `hostId` and cursor as `afterCursor`; it wakes on the first target that completes or needs attention, and timeout includes the latest commentary for all targets without waking on every commentary update. An up-to-date cursor suppresses already-delivered final text. Separate waits from one task may run serially. Do not narrate unchanged snapshots, and leave approval or user-input requests for the user.
- Only use `create_thread` when the user explicitly asks to create a new thread. Threads created this way are user-owned: they appear in the sidebar, and the user is expected to follow up with them directly. For subtasks of the current request, use multi-agent tools instead, including when the user explicitly asks for a subagent.
- After a successful `create_thread` call, emit `::created-thread{threadId="..."}` for a created thread or `::created-thread{clientThreadId="..."}` for queued worktree setup on its own line in your final response.

### Sidebar Organization
- Use `list_threads` to inspect pinned, custom, project, and task sidebar sections, and `list_projects` for project details. Use `create_sidebar_section`, `rename_sidebar_section`, `delete_sidebar_section`, `move_thread_to_sidebar_section`, `move_project_to_sidebar_section`, `reorder_sidebar_projects`, or `reorder_sidebar_sections` to organize tasks and projects. Moving an item into the pinned section pins it.

### Inline Code Comments
- Use the ::code-comment{...} directive when you need to attach feedback directly to specific code lines.
- Emit one directive per inline comment; emit none when there are no actionable inline comments.
- Required attributes: title (short label), body (one-paragraph explanation), file (path to the file).
- Optional attributes: start, end (1-based line numbers), priority (0-3).
- file should be an absolute path or include the workspace folder segment so it can be resolved relative to the workspace.
- Keep line ranges tight; end defaults to start.
- Example: ::code-comment{title="[P2] Off-by-one" body="Loop iterates past the end when length is 0." file="/path/to/foo.ts" start=10 end=11 priority=2}

### Inline Artifact Follow-Ups
- Format each artifact follow-up as an unescaped Markdown list item, `- :codex-followup[visible phrase]{prompt="Complete user request"}`; avoid closing brackets in the visible phrase and escape double quotes in the prompt.

### Git
- Branch prefix: `codex/`. Use this prefix by default when creating branches, but follow the user's request if they want a different prefix.
</app-context>

For requests to create or edit a standalone LaTeX document, use the built-in editor by default. Create or edit the .tex source with normal file tools, and open the saved file with open_in_codex unless it is already open or the user requests otherwise. Keep follow-up edits in that same file and editor. Use compile_latex_document after editing and fix source errors within its repair limits. Keep the editor open even when compilation fails; preserve the source and report unverified compilation or unsupported project requirements. Discover these tools if deferred. The native editor requires no LaTeX plugin or local TeX installation; do not install either for it. Ordinary math explanations stay in chat.

# AGENTS.md instructions for C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os

<INSTRUCTIONS>
# Global rules — Joshua

These apply on **every** project on this machine, in every session. Project-level
`AGENTS.md` files add to this; they do not replace it.

Keep this file short. It reloads on every message.

---

## Who

Joshua Fisseha — `contact@aspiringattorneys.com`. Builds **LSAT U** (lsatprep.study) and
the Aspiring Attorneys brand. Teaches the LSAT: ~25 one-to-one sessions a week plus
consultations. Windows 11; a MacBook Pro runs some sessions too, so paths differ between
them — never assume `/Users/joshuaf/...` resolves here.

## Where things live

- **AI OS / second brain:** `C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` — the Obsidian vault. `context/`,
  `wiki/`, `students/`, `corpus/`, `decisions/`, `brainstorms/`, `skills/`, `projects/`.
  Open this folder rather than starting with no folder, or memory cannot accumulate.
- **Skill catalog:** `dev/ai-os/skills/Codex` (179) and `skills/codex` (154), with
  `PARITY.md` declaring deliberate single-runtime skills and `INDEX.md` cataloging them.
- **LSAT U app:** repo `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db` — React + Vite + TS + Tailwind +
  shadcn, Supabase backend, hosted on Lovable.
- **GitHub:** account `vnjkoxpl-42905` (auto-generated name, correct account).

## How to work with him

- **Do the whole task.** Deliver the finished thing, then say plainly what you skipped
  and why. Don't hand back a plan when the work was asked for.
- **Look before asking.** If the answer is on disk or reachable through a connected tool,
  go get it. He has Firecrawl, Google Workspace (`gws`), and `gh` — use them.
- **Say what's actually true.** Distinguish verified from assumed, every time. If a step
  failed or was skipped, lead with that. A confident wrong answer costs more than "I
  don't know yet."
- **Surface your own mistakes immediately**, including scope errors you notice mid-task.
- Absolute dates (`YYYY-MM-DD`), never "yesterday" or "last week".
- Don't re-ask what he already answered. Don't pad with options he didn't request.

- **Dictation fixes.** A message shaped like `word --> word` (any arrow, any spacing), or
  "save X in my vocabulary", is a Superwhisper correction. Invoke the `superwhisper` skill
  and apply it in that turn, whatever else is in progress.

## Writing as Joshua

Anything published under his name — course copy, lessons, emails, posts.

Plain, direct, confident. Short sentences. Teach the reasoning rather than asserting the
conclusion; this is a teaching brand and the reasoning *is* the product. Concrete beats
abstract. Never condescend to the reader.

**Never use:** "delve into", "dive deep", "unlock", "unleash", "harness the power of",
"in today's fast-paced world", "ever-evolving landscape", "it's not just X, it's Y",
"navigate the complexities", "at the end of the day", "elevate", "supercharge",
"game-changer", "seamless", "robust", "I hope this helps!", "Great question!",
"Certainly!". No em-dash pile-ups. No paragraph opening with "Moreover," /
"Furthermore," / "Additionally,". No emoji in professional writing unless asked.

Headers should say something, not label a category — "Why students miss this", not
"Analysis".

## Safety

- **Draft by default; send only when he says "send".** Email, messages, posts are drafted
  and shown to him. When he says "send it" (or equivalent) in chat, send it, then log it in
  `wiki/send-ledger.md`. Never send on your own initiative, and never treat a request to
  draft as a request to send.
- **Never delete** files, emails, or records without explicit confirmation.
- The Google Workspace grant is broad (it includes deleting all Drive files), so the
  restraint has to live in tool permissions, not in wording. A prompt is not a permission
  layer.
- Secrets stay in `.env` and the OS keyring. Never paste a key into a note, a commit, or
  a chat.

## Tools already connected

- **Firecrawl** — CLI + 28 skills. Prefer it over MCP; the hosted MCP server was removed
  on purpose (the key would sit in plaintext in `~/.Codex.json`).
- **Google Workspace** — `gws` CLI, GCP project `ai-os-joshua`. Gmail, Calendar, Drive,
  Docs, Sheets, Slides, Tasks, People.
- **GitHub** — `gh` CLI, authenticated.

## Cost discipline

`AGENTS.md` reloads on every message, so keep this file lean. Prefer CLIs to MCP servers
(an MCP server can cost ~18k tokens per message in tool definitions). `/clear` between
unrelated tasks. Run the `handoff` skill past ~25% context rather than waiting for
auto-compact.

---

*Written 2026-09-07 from observed behavior. Correct it whenever it's wrong — that is the
point of the file.*

<!-- HAND-OWNED since 2026-07-17. This file is the authority for Codex — edit it directly. On any major change, mirror it into CLAUDE.md so Claude and Codex stay in sync. -->
# Jay 2 — operating instructions for Codex (ai-os)

The canonical root is the repository directory containing this file (`C:\Users\Joshu\dev\ai-os` on this Windows machine). Resolve paths from the repository root instead of assuming a saved Mac or Windows absolute path. This is the router: it holds pointers, not knowledge. Read it, then follow the routing map to the right file instead of searching blind. Twin for Claude Code: `CLAUDE.md`.

## Identity and mission

You are **Jay 2**, Joshua's executive assistant. This root is his EA workspace and command center. Joshua is the founder of Aspiring Attorneys and builder of LSAT U (lsatprep.study), a premium LSAT prep platform; he is the product, voice, and content authority. Your job: take operations, admin, drafting, and research off his plate so his time goes to teaching and building. Frame work as decisions and tradeoffs, not basics.

Standing duties, run from here without re-briefing: student operations (transcripts, assignments, follow-ups via `students/`), prospect and consultation correspondence (drafted in his voice, **never auto-sent unless a pre-approved template**), platform actions on lsatprep.study, session-note ingestion from Gmail/Drive, and keeping the wiki ledgers (`tasks`/`hot`/`log`) current so the next session starts oriented.

## Routing map

Paths are relative to the repository root.

| Need | Path |
|---|---|
| **Who Joshua is** (identity, preferences, contact) | `context/about-me.md` |
| **The business** (offers, money, channels, gaps) | `context/work.md` |
| **Current priorities** (the 90-day slate) | `context/priorities.md` |
| **What we decided and why** (locked decisions) | `decisions/log.md` |
| Freshest session state (hot cache) | `wiki/hot.md` |
| LLM Wiki schema (source, page, ingest/query/lint rules) | `system/schema/llm-wiki.md` |
| Master index · work ledger · dated history | `wiki/index.md` · `wiki/tasks.md` · `wiki/log.md` |
| Product ground truth (pointers to live backends) | `wiki/dashboard.md` |
| LSAT teaching knowledge | `corpus/index.md` |
| A student — profile, transcripts, work, correspondence | `students/<name>/` — start at `students/README.md` |
| Voice for anything a student or prospect reads | `wiki/teaching-voice.md` |
| Was it sent? (every outbound draft/send) | `wiki/send-ledger.md` — append-only; Gmail is authority |
| Who the students are (name, email, sessions, links) | `students/roster.generated.md` — regenerate, never hand-edit |
| Learned facts and preferences (automemory) | native store `~/.claude/projects/C--Users-Joshu-dev-ai-os/memory/` + `MEMORY.md` index |
| Skill / agent catalog | `skills/INDEX.md`, `agents/` |
| Systems Jay 2 can reach (registry) | `connections.md` — 7 data domains, mechanism/auth/freshness; read by `/audit` |
| Operator brain (how to think about automation) | `references/3ms-framework.md` — the Three Ms; walked weekly by `/level-up` |
| Automation build backlog (what to build next, ranked) | `references/automation-backlog.md` — corpus-derived gaps + tiers; feeds `/level-up` |
| How-it-works reference notes (methods, not state) | `references/` — also `references/claude-plus-codex.md`, `references/obsidian-command-center.md`, `references/second-brain-levels.md` |
| Web research and Firecrawl agents | `references/firecrawl.md` (guidelines in `references/firecrawl/`) |
| Immutable source evidence · intake queue | `raw/sources/` · `raw/inbox/` — one inbox, one archive |
| Archived web clips, with provenance + hashes | `raw/sources/clips/<slug>/` — clip + `PROVENANCE.md` + `SHA256SUMS` |
| Raw personal-data exports (Google Takeout, Gemini session transcripts) | `raw/data-about-me/` — unprocessed PII, git-ignored; never read wholesale |
| Design/plan thinking, dated (grill-me + interview outputs) | `brainstorms/` — supersedes the retired `aios/` reform folder |
| Session handoffs · chat exports | `handoffs/` — operational records, deliberately OUTSIDE `raw/`; `session-handoff` writes here |
| External teardowns and research, dated | `research/` |
| Web-clipped articles (Obsidian Web Clipper) | `raw/inbox/` — unprocessed only; `ingest` copies and hash-verifies them into `raw/sources/clips/`; processed originals stay until removal is authorized |
| Application code | `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` — own repo, own instructions, own remote |
| Other projects (life-path, …) | `../ai-os-projects/<name>/` — each with its own `CLAUDE.md` + `.claude/` |

Application repositories and dependencies stay outside this Obsidian vault, in sibling `../ai-os-projects/`. Do not create a junction from the vault back into that directory.

## Retrieval order

1. `wiki/hot.md` — current-session narrative and open threads.
2. `wiki/index.md` → the page (teaching knowledge: `corpus/index.md`; a student: their folder). Follow links; there is no semantic search and none is needed at this size.
3. The live system — the final freshness escalation.

**Live systems are authority for operational state; the brain mirrors them:** Cal.com + Google Calendar own sessions scheduled/used, LSAT U Supabase owns homework status/dossier/publish state, Stripe owns payment events, Gmail owns correspondence. On any disagreement, the backend wins. The brain is primary — nothing upstream holds them — for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, product specs, and decisions.

**Do not read the wiki unless the task needs it.** Retrieval costs context every turn. Skip it for: code edits in `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/`; shell/Git/file ops; questions the injected memories already answer; a skill run that names its own inputs; anything mechanical and self-contained. Route to it for: what is X · what did we decide and why · who a student is and where they stand · anything drafted in Joshua's voice for someone else · any decision touching roadmap, pedagogy, or UX.

**LLM Wiki schema:** For knowledge intake, knowledge questions, durable synthesis, and wiki maintenance, follow `system/schema/llm-wiki.md`. Use `ingest` as the single intake entry point; keep sources immutable, compile into existing pages, update the complete fenced catalog in `wiki/index.md`, and append changes to `wiki/log.md`. Preserve domain homes and live-system authority. Mechanical tasks retain the retrieval exceptions above.

**Wiki operations (LLM-wiki pattern):** the LLM writes/maintains `wiki/` and `corpus/`; Joshua sources, explores, asks. Raw sources are immutable — read, never modify. *Ingest:* read the source from `raw/`, discuss takeaways, write/update the pages it touches, update `wiki/index.md`, append to `wiki/log.md` (`## [YYYY-MM-DD] ingest | Title`); one source at a time, supervised, unless Joshua says batch. *Query:* index first, then pages; file valuable answers back as pages so explorations compound. *Lint:* `second-brain-lint` for contradictions, stale claims, orphans, missing pages.

## Voice

- **No em-dashes, ever.** Joshua never types them; a stray em-dash reads as AI and costs trust.
- **No AI-slop vocabulary** — never "delve," "tapestry," "testament," "demystify," "boasts," "in the realm of," and their kin.
- **Anything a student or prospect will read** routes through `wiki/teaching-voice.md` and is drafted, never sent, unless it is a pre-approved template.
- **When an outbound message is authorized**, preserve the draft's paragraph breaks, list lines, and intentional spacing. After sending, compare the recipient-facing rendering with the draft and confirm exactly one copy landed. Report any mismatch; never resend automatically.
- **Lead with your recommendation, not a menu.** Be decisive; don't over-hedge.
- **Any prompt Joshua will paste elsewhere** goes inside a fenced code block, never inline prose.

## Tools, keys, and secrets

- **The entire secret model is `.env` + `.gitignore`.** The root `.env` (git-ignored — verify with `git check-ignore .env`) is Joshua's single central store for **everything**: API keys, passwords, and bank/account info (decided 2026-07-17). OAuth client secrets live in `~/.config/gws/`, outside the repo. There is no vault, no Keychain, no encrypted store (`records.sparsebundle` is dead) and none is wanted. Never paste a credential into chat — add it to `.env` directly in the editor.
- **Safety is the tool layer, not prompts:** allow/deny tool lists + scoped API keys. Jay 2 acts under its own credentials and never impersonates Joshua.
- **Connected live systems:** Cal.com, Google Calendar, LSAT U Supabase, Stripe, Gmail. Full registry in `connections.md`.
- **Google access is `gws` CLI only** (`gws gmail|drive|calendar|docs …`, keyring-auth; see `skills/codex/gws-shared/`). Never the Google connector MCPs — the CLI is faster, batchable, and exports Docs to markdown.
- **Government IDs stay outside this OS.** Everything else (student names, emails, schedules, transcripts, notes, payment *links*) is ordinary local working material — classify by content, never by filename.
- **Git remote is enabled.** `origin` is GitHub (`vnjkoxpl-42905/ai-os`, decided 2026-09-29). The outer repo never tracks `.env`, `../ai-os-projects/lsatu/` (own repo), runtime logs, or locks. Do not create gitlinks.
- **New laptop restore:** GitHub contains tracked ai-os files and skills. It deliberately excludes `.env`, `raw/`, app repos, credentials, and machine sessions. Restore machine-specific files from the private backup using `setup/backup/README.md` and its `README - START HERE.md`; install tools separately and sign in again where sessions cannot be restored. Never move credentials, raw personal data, or active browser tokens into GitHub.

## Skills

Everything is a skill — **there are no slash commands** (decided 2026-07-17: one kind, one home, one activation path). The catalog at `skills/` and `agents/` is inert storage; nothing auto-loads. An item runs only once **activated by copy** (not symlink) into `.agents/skills/` (Claude: `.claude/skills/`). Creating one writes to **both** the project and the catalog (`skills/{claude,codex}/`), project-scoped — never auto-global. `claude/` and `codex/` subtrees are never merged; a vendor item is a live symlink + a frozen `_snapshots/` copy. `system/scripts/check-skills.sh` validates the model; `skills/INDEX.md` indexes it.

Active in this root. Run `pickup` only when Joshua explicitly asks for it. Do not run it automatically at session start. Use `wrapup` at the end of substantive sessions:

| Skill | Use it when |
|---|---|
| `pickup` / `wrapup` | `pickup` only on Joshua's explicit request / `wrapup` at session end (wrapup persists to the wiki ledgers) |
| `session-handoff` | mid-work context reset near ~250k, or passing work Codex↔Claude — chat-only, writes nothing |
| `audit` | scoring the OS against the Four Cs (structural health, /100) |
| `level-up` | weekly 3Ms interview to find + ship one automation |
| `consultation-inquiry-drafts`, `-followup-drafts`, `inbound-tutoring-replies` | drafting to prospects/students — always via the teaching voice |
| `guided-drill-assignment` | assigning a student a drill |
| `lsat-u-platform-actions` | doing anything ON lsatprep.study from here (assign, post, message, ingest) |
| `tutorocean-profile-fill` | TutorOcean profile work |
| `prompt-architect-refiner` | refining a prompt before it ships |
| `accommodations-email` | "accommodations email to X" (dictation: "accommodations scale"); pre-approved send, QP form attached, self-logging |

When a skill run exposes a gap, improve the skill before moving on. Runtime-specific catalog differences must be justified in `skills/PARITY.md`; undeclared differences are defects.

## Guardrails

- **95% rule.** Make no changes until you are 95% confident what Joshua wants. Ask until you are.
- **Lovable credits require confirmation.** Never take an action that spends or may spend Lovable credits without Joshua's explicit confirmation immediately before the action. Do not infer approval from a broader request to fix, build, deploy, or continue. Free Lovable controls and direct deployment paths may be used without this credit-spend confirmation.
- **Lovable Cloud deploys through Lovable.** LSAT U currently uses a Lovable Cloud-managed backend. Deploy its Edge Functions through the Lovable agent, not the Supabase CLI, unless the backend has first been migrated to a separately managed Supabase project. Confirm this route with Joshua before starting a Lovable deployment.
- **Root stays thin.** Don't accumulate files at the root; work products go to their homes (wiki, students, corpus, projects). Anything left at root gets filed before the session ends.
- **Delegate bulk work.** Exploration or analysis spanning 3+ files goes to a sub-agent that returns summaries, not file dumps.
- **Shell ergonomics.** Run one command per call — chained `cmd; cmd` and `rm -rf X && clone Y` forms trip permission denials that the split commands would not. Never poll with `sleep`. Use backgrounded execution for anything slow, and raise the timeout explicitly for known-slow ops (large moves, typecheck-gated pushes, full verify runs) rather than eating the default.
- **Search before creating.** One authoritative page per subject — update it, don't grow a parallel truth. `wiki/index.md` catalogs every page and updates on every ingest.
- **Preserve provenance and source hashes.** Never move, delete, or modify a migration source root — copy and hash-verify. Never follow a source symlink outside its registered root.
- **Conflicts stay explicit until adjudicated** — never silently overwrite one source generation with another.
- **Automation writes only generated files or fenced generated sections,** through compare-and-swap.
- **Obey nested instruction files** — they win over broader ones for their subtree. LSATU's recovered `CLAUDE.md`/`AGENTS.md` are inviolate: never regenerate them without Joshua's explicit approval.
- **Memory = native automemory.** "Remember that…" writes one fact per file to the native store; `MEMORY.md` indexes it. This file is the rules; memory is learned facts — never mix them. Transcripts and raw captures never auto-promote.
- **Append meaningful system changes** to `wiki/log.md`; log locked decisions to `decisions/log.md`.

## Dual-runtime (Codex + Claude)

- Begin at the Git-root `AGENTS.md` and follow directory-scoped instruction files toward the working path; the nearest file wins for its subtree.
- Keep root instructions inside the runtime byte budget. Move narrow rules to the narrowest owning directory rather than growing this file.
- Activated skills are **copies** in `.agents/skills/` (from `skills/codex/`); agent definitions convert to `.codex/agents/*.toml` with the instructions in `developer_instructions`.
- Claude reads `CLAUDE.md` and never this file. Anything that must hold for both runtimes goes in BOTH files — when you change it here, mirror it there; keep the routing map and guardrails byte-identical between them.
- Activation is unequal by design: Codex reads copies in `.agents/skills/`, Claude reads copies in `.claude/skills/`. Codex and Claude both activate the LLM Wiki ingest, query, and lint workflows. Other runtime-specific activation differences remain declared in `skills/PARITY.md`. Never have separate runtimes writing the same page concurrently.
- Never have Claude and Codex editing the same file at once; pass work between them by session handoff (summarize → clear → paste). At ~250k tokens, hand off rather than riding autocompact.

<multi_agent_role>You are an agent in a team of agents collaborating to complete a task.

You can spawn sub-agents to handle subtasks, and those sub-agents can spawn their own sub-agents. All agents in the team, including the agents that you can assign tasks to, are equally intelligent and capable, and have access to the same set of tools.

You can use `spawn_agent` to create a new agent, `followup_task` to give an existing agent a new task and trigger a turn, and `send_message` to pass a message to a running agent.
`send_message` calls may be read by a human, so ensure they are legible. Always put proper spaces between words and/or numbers.
Child agents can also spawn their own sub-agents.

When you provide a response in the final channel, that content is immediately delivered back to your parent agent.
In addition, your final answer may be read by a human, so ensure it is legible.

You will receive messages in the analysis channel in the form:
```
Message Type: NEW_TASK | MESSAGE | FINAL_ANSWER
Task name: <recipient>
Sender: <author>
Payload:
<payload text>
```
You may also see them addressed as to=/root/..., which indicates your identity is /root/...

Note that collaboration tools cannot be called from inside `functions.exec`. Call `spawn_agent`, `send_message`, `followup_task`, `wait_agent`, `interrupt_agent`, and `list_agents` only as direct tool calls using the recipient shown in their tool definitions, such as `to=functions.collaboration.spawn_agent`, since they are intentionally absent from the `functions.exec` `tools.*` namespace. Available tools in `functions.exec` are explicitly described with a `tools` namespace in the developer message.

All agents share the same directory. In detail:
- All agents have access to the same container and filesystem as you.
- All agents use the same current working directory.
- As a result, edits made by one agent are immediately visible to all other agents.

When calling `wait_agent`, prefer longer waits (minutes) to avoid busy polling.

There are 4 available concurrency slots, meaning that up to 4 agents can be active at once, including you.

Full-history forks (`fork_turns` omitted or `"all"`) inherit the parent model and reasoning effort and do not accept overrides. Only set `model` or `reasoning_effort` when explicitly requested by the user, applicable `AGENTS.md` instructions, or skill instructions; when doing so, set `fork_turns` to `"none"` or a positive integer string.</multi_agent_role>

});\\r\\n    if (error) throw error;\\r\\n    return json({ received: true, booking_id: result?.booking_id, status: result?.status });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n\\r\\n  // Existing package branch, now protected Provider event ids are the idempotency\\r\\n-- boundary: a webhook retry must never add package sessions twice.\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_offers (\\r\\n  id text PRIMARY KEY,\\r\\n  label text NOT NULL,\\r\\n  provider text NOT NULL CHECK (provider IN (\'stripe\', \'calcom\')),\\r\\n  provider_ref text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\\r\\n  url text NOT NULL,\\r\\n  credits integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (credits >= 0),\\r\\n  active boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\\r\\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nINSERT INTO public.booking_offers (id, label, provider, provider_ref, url, credits)\\r\\nVALUES\\r\\n  (\'single-session\', \'Single session\', \'calcom\', \'session-link\', \'https://cal.com/joshua-jtx5ud/session-link\', 0),\\r\\n  (\'package-5\', \'5-hour package\', \'stripe\', \'plink_1STvqYIULVCj6rqqJb70Prhb\', \'https://buy.stripe.com/4gMaEY05F3dc9x13NqdZ61x\', 5),\\r\\n  (\'package-10\', \'10-hour package\', \'stripe\', \'plink_1TvTs5IULVCj6rqqZo7FG76I\', \'https://buy.stripe.com/3cI6oI2dN5lk4cHcjWdZ61O\', 10)\\r\\nON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE SET label = excluded.label, provider = excluded.provider,\\r\\n  provider_ref = excluded.provider_ref, url = excluded.url, credits = excluded.credits;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.student_booking_offers (\\r\\n  student_id uuid NOT NULL REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\\r\\n  offer_id text NOT NULL REFERENCES public.booking_offers(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\\r\\n  enabled boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT true,\\r\\n  url_override text,\\r\\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  PRIMARY KEY (student_id, offer_id)\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.booking_commerce_events (\\r\\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\r\\n  student_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\\r\\n  offer_id text REFERENCES public.booking_offers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\\r\\n  provider text NOT NULL CHECK (provider IN (\'stripe\', \'calcom\', \'lsatu\')),\\r\\n  provider_event_id text NOT NULL,\\r\\n  event_type text NOT NULL,\\r\\n  email text,\\r\\n  amount_cents integer,\\r\\n  currency text,\\r\\n  payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\\r\\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  UNIQUE (provider, provider_event_id, event_type)\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.external_booking_events (\\r\\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\r\\n  student_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\\r\\n  offer_id text REFERENCES public.booking_offers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\\r\\n  provider text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'calcom\',\\r\\n  provider_booking_id text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\\r\\n  email text,\\r\\n  attendee_name text,\\r\\n  start_at timestamptz,\\r\\n  end_at timestamptz,\\r\\n  status text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'booked\',\\r\\n  source_url text,\\r\\n  google_event_id text,\\r\\n  schedule_event_id uuid REFERENCES public.schedule_events(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\\r\\n  payload jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT \'{}\'::jsonb,\\r\\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\r\\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\\r\\n);\\r\\n\\r\\nGRANT SELECT ON public.booking_offers TO authenticated;\\r\\nGRANT SELECT ON public.student_booking_offers TO authenticated;\\r\\nGRANT SELECT ON public.booking_commerce_events TO authenticated;\\r\\nGRANT SELECT ON public.external_booking_events TO authenticated;\\r\\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_offers TO service_role;\\r\\nGRANT ALL ON public.student_booking_offers TO service_role;\\r\\nGRANT ALL ON public.booking_commerce_events TO service_role;\\r\\nGRANT ALL ON public.external_booking_events TO service_role;\\r\\n\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.booking_offers ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.student_booking_offers ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.booking_commerce_events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\nALTER TABLE public.external_booking_events ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"authenticated_read_active_booking_offers\\" ON public.booking_offers\\r\\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (active);\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"students_read_own_booking_offers\\" ON public.student_booking_offers\\r\\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (student_id = auth.uid());\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"students_read_own_booking_commerce\\" ON public.booking_commerce_events\\r\\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (student_id = auth.uid());\\r\\nCREATE POLICY \\"students_read_own_external_bookings\\" ON public.external_booking_events\\r\\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (student_id = auth.uid());\\r\\n\\r\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.apply_booking_package_payment(\\r\\n  _student_id uuid,\\r\\n  _offer_id text,\\r\\n  _provider_event_id text,\\r\\n  _email text DEFAULT NULL\\r\\n) RETURNS public.booking_passes\\r\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\r\\nDECLARE\\r\\n  _offer public.booking_offers%ROWTYPE;\\r\\n  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;\\r\\nBEGIN\\r\\n  SELECT * INTO _offer FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _offer_id AND provider = \'stripe\' AND active;\\r\\n  IF NOT FOUND OR _offer.credits <= 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'not_a_package_offer\'; END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.booking_commerce_events WHERE provider = \'stripe\' AND provider_event_id = _provider_event_id AND event_type = \'paid\') THEN\\r\\n    SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = _student_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1;\\r\\n    RETURN _pass;\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = _student_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE;\\r\\n  IF FOUND THEN\\r\\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes\\r\\n      SET total_credits = total_credits + _offer.credits,\\r\\n          status = \'active\', expires_at = NULL\\r\\n      WHERE id = _pass.id RETURNING * INTO _pass;\\r\\n  ELSE\\r\\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_passes (student_id, student_email, student_name, total_credits, used_credits, session_length_minutes, status)\\r\\n      VALUES (_student_id, _email, COALESCE(_email, \'Student\'), _offer.credits, 0, 70, \'active\')\\r\\n      RETURNING * INTO _pass;\\r\\n  END IF;\\r\\n\\r\\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (student_id, offer_id, provider, provider_event_id, event_type, email)\\r\\n    VALUES (_student_id, _offer_id, \'stripe\', _provider_event_id, \'paid\', _email);\\r\\n  RETURN _pass;\\r\\nEND;\\r\\n$$;\\r\\n\\r\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.apply_booking_package_payment(uuid, text, text, text) TO service_role;\\r\\n"}'}]

{'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"cf4177","wall_time_seconds":6.0565657,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":925,"output":"# Native Single Session workers\\r\\n\\r\\nThe database migrations are stage 1 only. They create worker RPCs, leases, and\\r\\nheartbeats, but intentionally do not put an invocation secret in SQL or cron\\r\\nmetadata.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Stage 2 provisioning\\r\\n\\r\\nAt deploy time, use the Supabase Management API to set the same randomly\\r\\ngenerated `BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET` function secret on these deployed functions:\\r\\n\\r\\n- `single-session-sweeper`\\r\\n- `single-session-outbox`\\r\\n- `gcal-reconcile`\\r\\n\\r\\nThe outbox and sweeper also require `STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`; both booking workers\\r\\nrequire the existing Supabase function secrets. The outbox requires\\r\\n`SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY`, `SUPABASE_ANON_KEY`, and\\r\\n`BOOKING_ADMIN_EMAIL`. The public endpoint requires `TURNSTILE_SECRET`,\\r\\n`BOOKING_IP_HMAC_KEY`, `SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY`, and\\r\\n`STRIPE_SECRET_KEY`. The commerce webhook requires `STRIPE_LIVEMODE` set\\r\\nexplicitly to `true` or `false`, plus its Stripe signing secret. Missing secrets\\r\\nproduce a non-2xx response.\\r\\n\\r\\nInstall authenticated scheduler calls outside the migration.\\r\\n\\r\\n> **Nothing below is installed as of 2026-09-04, and the table is a\\r\\n> specification rather than a description.** `cron.job` in this project holds\\r\\n> four jobs and all four are booking and calendar work; Cloud Scheduler holds\\r\\n> three paused transcript jobs; there is no launchd agent. In particular\\r\\n> `sweep-rrc-grading` has no scheduler at all, so the safety net ADR 0021\\r\\n> describes does not exist. Verified by reading `cron.job` directly. Anything\\r\\n> that says otherwise is describing this table rather than the deployment.\\r\\n>\\r\\n> Installing one is `cron.schedule` with a `net.http_post`, the shape the four\\r\\n> live jobs already use. It needs a decision about where the worker secret\\r\\n> lives first: the existing jobs carry a Bearer [REDACTED] inline in the command\\r\\n> text, which the rotation section below is written against.\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n| Worker | Suggested interval | Request |\\r\\n| --- | --- | --- |\\r\\n| `single-session-sweeper` | every minute | `POST /functions/v1/single-session-sweeper` |\\r\\n| `single-session-outbox` | every minute | `POST /functions/v1/single-session-outbox` |\\r\\n| `gcal-reconcile` | every five minutes | `POST /functions/v1/gcal-reconcile` |\\r\\n| `sweep-rrc-grading` | every ten minutes | `POST /functions/v1/sweep-rrc-grading` |\\r\\n\\r\\nEach request carries `X-Worker-Secret` from the scheduler\'s secret store. Do\\r\\nnot place its plaintext value in a SQL migration, cron command, or repository\\r\\nfile.\\r\\n\\r\\n`sweep-rrc-grading` is not a booking worker and writes no heartbeat, so it is\\r\\nabsent from `booking_worker_heartbeats` and from `get_booking_health()` by\\r\\ndesign: a Read, Recall, Colour grade that has not been swept is not a booking\\r\\noutage. It shares `BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET` because a deployment has one worker\\r\\nsecret, and needs nothing else -- it reaches the grader with the service role\\r\\nkey the platform already gives it. Its own health is the `deferred` count in\\r\\nits JSON response: a number that climbs run after run means sections are\\r\\narriving faster than three per ten minutes, which no single tutor\'s students\\r\\ncan do.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Rotation\\r\\n\\r\\n1. Generate a new high-entropy value in the deployment secret manager.\\r\\n2. Update the Edge Function secret and scheduler request secret together.\\r\\n3. Trigger each worker once and confirm a 2xx response.\\r\\n4. Remove the old value from the deployment system.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Post-deploy assertion\\r\\n\\r\\nConfirm all three schedules exist in the deployment scheduler, trigger them,\\r\\nthen inspect `booking_worker_heartbeats`. Each expected worker must have a\\r\\nrecent `last_success_at`. `get_booking_health()` must report zero stale workers.\\r\\nThe deployment is incomplete until those assertions pass.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"258ed0","wall_time_seconds":5.7288409,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":290,"output":"docs/adr/0005-single-session-native-slot-first.md:8:The booking-unification plan (2026-08-13) locked \\"single session stays on Cal.com; no Stripe single-session product.\\" Since then: (a) the native booking core (availability, slot engine, Google Calendar + Meet creation, confirmation email) has proven itself for Packages; (b) the Cal.com account has zero webhooks configured, so Cal bookings never reach the app — they surface as \\"Nothing booked\\" on every student page; (c) Joshua wants one machine, not a mind split across two systems.\\r\\ndocs/adr/0005-single-session-native-slot-first.md:12:1. **Pay-first (1-credit pass):** buy via Payment Link, webhook grants a pass, booker then books on their pass link. Least new code, but a Stripe Payment Link\'s success redirect is static, so the buyer must leave the flow and find an email to finish — worse than the Cal.com experience being replaced.\\r\\ndocs/adr/0005-single-session-native-slot-first.md:26:- The Cal.com webhook receiver branch in `booking-commerce-webhook` remains dead code until Cal is retired or a webhook is finally registered.\\r\\nsupabase/config.toml:39:[functions.booking-commerce-webhook]\\n"}'}]

path.resolve(path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), \\"../..\\");\\r\\nfunction readEnv(f) {\\r\\n  if (!fs.existsSync(f)) return {};\\r\\n  const out = {};\\r\\n  for (const line of fs.readFileSync(f, \\"utf8\\").split(/\\\\r?\\\\n/)) {\\r\\n    const t = line.trim();\\r\\n    if (!t || t.startsWith(\\"#\\") || !t.includes(\\"=\\")) continue;\\r\\n    const i = t.indexOf(\\"=\\");\\r\\n    out[t.slice(0, i).trim()] = t.slice(i + 1).trim().replace(/^[\\"\']|[\\"\']$/g, \\"\\");\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  return out;\\r\\n}\\r\\nconst env = { ...readEnv(projectPath(\'lsatu\', \'.env\')), ...readEnv(path.join(ROOT, \\".env\\")) };\\r\\nconst URL_ = env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL;\\r\\nconst KEY = env.VITE_SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst argv[2] : undefined;\\r\\nconst flag = (n, d) => { const i = argv.indexOf(`--${n}`); return i >= 0 ? argv[i + 1] : d; };\\r\\nconst has = (n) => argv.includes(`--${n}`);\\r\\nconst dry = }\\r\\n    else {\\r\\n      const current = previous.find((item) => item.content_html === html);\\r\\n      let result;\\r\\n      if (current) result = { ok: true, item_id: current.id, route: `/classroom/item/${current.id}`, already_current: true, notified: false };\\r\\n      else {\\r\\n        // Archived copies count as prior generations. Reusing an old RPC key\\r\\n        // would return the archived row instead of creating a visible copy.\\r\\n        const generation = sameLesson.filter((item) => item.status === \\"archived\\").length;\\r\\n        const key = `interactive-${createHash(\\"sha256\\").update(u.class_id).update(lessonId).update(title).update(html).update(String(generation)).digest(\\"hex\\").slice(0, 48)}`;\\r\\n        result = await rest(\\"rpc/mcp_post_classroom_note\\", {\\r\\n          method: \\"POST\\",\\r\\n          body: JSON.stringify({\\r\\n            p_student_id: u.class_id,\\r\\n            p_title: title,\\r\\n            p_content_html: html,\\r\\n            p_idempotency_key: key,\\r\\n            p_description: `Interactive lesson [${lessonId}]: ${title}`,\\r\\n            p_notify: true,\\r\\n          }),\\r\\n        });\\r\\n      }\\r\\n      if (!result?.ok || !result?.item_id) throw new Error(\\"LSAT U did not confirm the new lesson\\");\\r\\n      const [posted] = await rest(`classroom_items?id=eq.${result.item_id}&student_id=eq.${u.class_id}&select=id,title,status,visible_to_student,content_html`);\\r\\n      if (!posted?.visible_to_student || posted.status === \\"archived\\" || posted.content_html !== html) {\\r\\n        throw new Error(`New item ${result.item_id} did not verify; Nothing here ever revokes — a refund is Joshua\'s call from People,\\r\\n// not something a webhook should decide.\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const PAID_STUDENT_FLAGS = [\\r\\n  \\"has_classroom_access\\",\\r\\n  \\"has_practice_access\\",\\r\\n  \\"has_drill_access\\",\\r\\n  \\"has_bootcamp_access\\",\\r\\n  \\"has_journal_access\\",\\r\\n  \\"has_waj_access\\",\\r\\n  \\"has_chat_access\\",\\r\\n  \\"has_schedule_access\\",\\r\\n  \\"has_activity_access\\",\\r\\n] as const;\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type GrantResult =\\r\\n  | { granted: true; class_id: string }\\r\\n  | { granted: false; reason: \\"no_email\\" | \\"no_account\\" };\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Opens the paid feature set for whoever owns `email`. */\\r\\nexport async function grantStudentAccess(\\r\\n  admin: { auth: { admin: { listUsers: (opts: { perPage: number }) => Promise<any> } }; from: (t: string) => any },\\r\\n  email: string | null | undefined,\\r\\n): Promise<GrantResult> {\\r\\n  if (!email) return { granted: false, reason: \\"no_email\\" };\\r\\n  const target = email.trim().toLowerCase();\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { data } = await admin.auth.admin.listUsers({ perPage: 1000 });\\r\\n  const user = (data?.users ?? []).find(\\r\\n    (u: { email?: string | null }) => (u.email ?? \\"\\").toLowerCase() === target,\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  // Someone can pay before they ever make an account. That is not an error:\\r\\n  // Joshua grants them by hand once they sign up, and the payment record still\\r\\n  // exists either way.\\r\\n  if (!user) return { granted: false, reason: \\"no_account\\" };\\r\\n\\r\\n  const updates: Record<string, boolean> = {};\\r\\n  for (const flag of PAID_STUDENT_FLAGS) updates[flag] = true;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const { error } = await admin.from(\\"profiles\\").update(updates).eq(\\"class_id\\", user.id);\\r\\n  if (error) throw error;\\r\\n  return { granted: true, class_id: user.id };\\r\\n}\\r\\n"}'}]

values are redacted before any DB row reaches the model.\\r\\nconst PII_COLUMN_RE = /(email|phone|password|secret|token|api_?key|ip_?addr|address|ssn|dob|birth)/i;\\r\\n// Tables the inspector may never sample, regardless of request.\\r\\nconst TABLE_DENYLIST = new Set([\\r\\n  \\"user_roles\\",\\r\\n  \\"auth\\",\\r\\n  \\"secrets\\",\\r\\n  \\"billing\\",\\r\\n  \\"payments\\",\\r\\n]);\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface ToolCall {\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n  type: \\"function\\";\\r\\n  function: { name: string; arguments: string };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\ninterface string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction json(data: unknown, status = 200) {\\r\\n  return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), {\\r\\n    status,\\r\\n    headers: { ...corsHeaders, \\"Content-Type\\": \\"application/json\\" },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function verifyAdmin(req: Request) {\\r\\n  const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get(\\"SUPABASE_URL\\")!;\\r\\n  const anonKey = Deno.env.get(\\"SUPABASE_ANON_KEY\\")!;\\r\\n  const serviceRoleKey = Deno.env.get(\\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\\")!;\\r\\n\\r\\n  const authHeader = req.headers.get(\\"Authorization\\");\\r\\n  if (!authHeader) throw new Error(\\"Missing auth\\");\\r\\n\\r\\n  const anonClient = createClient(supabaseUrl, anonKey, {\\r\\n    global: { headers: { Authorization: [REDACTED] } },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  const {\\r\\n    data: { user },\\r\\n    error,\\r\\n  } = await anonClient.auth.getUser();\\r\\n  if (error || !user) throw new Error(\\"Unauthorized\\");\\r\\n\\r\\n  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey);\\r\\n  const { data: roleData } = await admin\\r\\n    .from(\\"user_roles\\")\\r\\n    .select(\\"role\\")\\r\\n    .eq(\\"user_id\\", user.id)\\r\\n    .eq(\\"role\\", \\"admin\\")\\r\\n    .maybeSingle();\\r\\n\\r\\n  if (!roleData) throw new Error(\\"Forbidden\\");\\r\\n  return { admin, callerId: user.id };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n// ---- GitHub read helpers (all read-only) -----------------------------------\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function ghFetch(path: string, token: string) {\\r\\n  const res = await fetch(`https://api.github.com${path}`, {\\r\\n    headers: {\\r\\n      Authorization: [REDACTED] ${token}`,\\r\\n      Accept: \\"application/vnd.github+json\\",\\r\\n      \\"User-Agent\\": \\"lsatu-inspector\\",\\r\\n    },\\r\\n  });\\r\\n  return res;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nasync function toolListFiles(args: { path?: string }, token: string): Promise<string> {\\r\\n  // Use the git tree API for a full recursive listing from a subtree, or the\\r\\n  // contents API for a single directory. Default: recursive tree from root.\\r\\n  const path = (args.path ?? \\"\\").replace(/^\\\\/+|\\\\/+$/g, \\"\\");\\r\\n  if (!path) {\\r\\n    const res = await ghFetch(`/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/git/trees/HEAD?recursive=1`, token);\\r\\n    if (!res.ok) return `error: list_files failed (${res.status})`;\\r\\n    const data = await res.json();\\r\\n    const paths = (data.tree ?? [])\\r\\n      .filter((t: { type: string }) => t.type === \\"blob\\")\\r\\n      .map((t: { path: string }) => t.path);\\r\\n    return JSON.stringify({ truncated: data.truncated ?? false, files: paths });\\r\\n  }\\r\\n  const res = await ghFetch(\\r\\n    `/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/contents/${encodeURIComponent(path).replace(/%2F/g, \\"/\\")}`,\\r\\n    token\\r\\n  );\\r\\n  if (!res.ok) return `error: list_files failed for \\"${path}\\" (${res.status})`;\\r\\n  const data = await res.json();\\r\\n  if (Array.isArray(data)) {\\r\\n    return JSON.stringify(\\r\\n      data.map((e: { path: string; type: string }) => ({ path: e.path, type: e.type }))\\r\\n"}'}]

text(await tools.apply_patch("*** Begin Patch\n*** Add File: C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/research/2026-10-02-celia-payment-crediting.md\n+# Celia paid for five sessions, but the crediting failure needs delivery evidence\n+\n+Investigated 2026-10-02 through direct authenticated Stripe API reads, authenticated LSAT U admin reads, a safe unsigned endpoint probe, and the local app source. No payment event was replayed and no app code was changed.\n+\n+## Verified payment and manual restoration\n+\n+- Student: Celia, `celiacutler@gmail.com`, account `749e2eae-7969-464f-bf96-a4d4cfafe2bf`.\n+- Completed payment: USD 200.00, live mode, at `2026-10-01T23:39:21Z` (2026-10-01 19:39:21 America/Toronto).\n+- Event: `evt_1ULtZOIULVCj6rqqOYGA3CEa`.\n+- Checkout: `cs_live_a1uWZ6oEgv2q3RdBlE51zR8EUSiWB9ygxH2nlZs1oqfv8wAwG6tLxcQpek`.\n+- Payment intent: `pi_3ULtZLIULVCj6rqq0akU648V`.\n+- Payment link: `plink_1STvqYIULVCj6rqqJb70Prhb`. This matches active live offer `package-5`, worth five credits.\n+- Checkout email matches her account exactly. Current payment-link line-item total is also USD 200.00. Checkout has no discount, shipping, or tax adjustment.\n+- Parent agent independently restored and verified active pass `014de9d0-5d10-45da-80be-483c01aaba35`, created `2026-10-02T17:18:47Z`, five total and zero used. Earlier ten-credit pass remains exhausted.\n+\n+## What the evidence rules out\n+\n+Stripe endpoint `we_1U9Sw2IULVCj6rqqkkoe7Isw` is enabled and targets `https://atrcqxpmqrynmtvvyjju.supabase.co/functions/v1/booking-commerce-webhook?provider=stripe`. It subscribes to `checkout.session.completed`, among other events.\n+\n+An unsigned POST with body `{}` returned HTTP 400 `invalid_signature`, confirming the function is reachable without a JWT and performs signature validation. This does not test valid-signature delivery.\n+\n+The payment event reports zero pending webhooks. This means no outstanding deliveries, **not proof of successful processing**. The precise failure remains unverified because delivery response logs, deployed function source, and service-role payment-ledger access were unavailable in this investigation. Admin REST returned no commerce rows, but local RLS grants students only their own rows; that empty response cannot establish an empty ledger.\n+\n+## What the local implementation does\n+\n+`../ai-os-projects/lsatu/supabase/functions/booking-commerce-webhook/index.ts` checks completed payment, live mode, known active payment link, amount and currency, then matches the account by email. Celia's verified facts satisfy the visible package checks. A successful match calls `apply_booking_package_payment`.\n+\n+`../ai-os-projects/lsatu/supabase/migrations/20260819075740_aaf80471-b8b9-452a-8713-02cc380f7608.sql` defines that RPC. It increments the newest pass or inserts one and records a `paid` commerce event in the same transaction. Event ID is its idempotency boundary. Its authenticated execution grant was subsequently revoked; admin JWT cannot invoke it directly.\n+\n+Local gaps:\n+\n+- Unknown payment links return HTTP 200 with `ignored: unknown_payment_link`, without a durable diagnostic record.\n+- Unmatched email creates `unmatched_paid` and returns HTTP 200 pending, but no package reconciliation worker exists locally.\n+- `checkout.session.async_payment_succeeded` is subscribed but ignored by the handler; a delayed successful payment would not receive credits.\n+- Successful package ledger records omit checkout session, amount, and currency despite schema support, which limits diagnosis and reconciliation.\n+- Admins have no visible commerce-ledger policy in the inspected migrations, making the existing REST read uninformative.\n+- Validation uses the payment link's current price/active state rather than the purchase-time facts, so later price changes or link deactivation can reject a valid purchase replay.\n+\n+## Recommended resolution\n+\n+First inspect the delivery of Celia's exact event in Stripe and the function's corresponding response/error, then read its commerce rows using an authorized service-role or admin diagnostic endpoint. Confirm which deployed function version ran. The local logic should have credited this exact payment, so changing email matching or offer mapping would be speculative.\n+\n+Protect the manual restoration from duplicate credits before retries: reconcile the original event to the already granted pass and record it as fulfilled through a transaction that does not grant more sessions. **Do not replay Celia's event or run the normal credit RPC now**, because the manual pass has no Stripe event-id marker.\n+\n+Add an admin payment diagnostic/reconciliation surface, durable paid-payment receipt records keyed by checkout session/payment intent, and a retry worker that reconciles unfulfilled receipts. Handle delayed payment success, unknown offers, and unmatched students explicitly. Surface actionable failures to Joshua without sending student messages automatically.\n+\n+Meaningful verification should cover duplicate event delivery, manual fulfillment followed by delayed retry, unmatched email resolved after signup, unknown link remediation, delayed payment success, price changes after purchase, and transactional rollback when credit or receipt persistence fails. These changes involve Edge Functions/database work and require the approved Lovable deployment route before being called live.\n*** End Patch"));

## assistant

Verified Celia’s USD $200 payment for five sessions on 2026-10-01, event `evt_1ULtZOIULVCj6rqqOYGA3CEa`. Account email, active payment link, and amount all match. Stripe’s endpoint is enabled and reachable without JWT.

Exact failure remains unresolved pending delivery response logs or privileged commerce-ledger access. Local code should have credited this payment. Zero pending webhooks does not prove successful processing.

Do not replay her event: the manually restored five-credit pass lacks a Stripe fulfillment marker and could receive another five credits.

Saved evidence and concrete remediation in [investigation report](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os/research/2026-10-02-celia-payment-crediting.md). Recommended fix: reconcile the original event to the manual pass without adding credits, then add durable payment receipts, admin diagnostics, and reconciliation for missed or unmatched payments.

Get the feel for it: the early attribution, the pivot, the author stepping in to say \\\\\\"no, here\'s what\'s actually true.\\\\\\" That sentiment that sounds like \\\\\\"they\'re wrong\\\\\\" is your conclusion.</p>\\\\n\\\\n  <div class=\\\\\\"example\\\\\\">\\\\n    <span class=\\\\\\"tag\\\\\\">Worked example (the tomato)</span>\\\\n    \\\\\\"Tomatoes grow on a vine to about the size of a baseball. Most people believe that tomatoes are vegetables, since tomatoes are often found in salads and savory dishes. But they are wrong. After all, although tomatoes are never found in fruit salads, tomatoes have seeds and so they are fruit.\\\\\\"<br><br>\\\\n    <b>Background:</b> Tomatoes grow on a vine, baseball-sized.<br>\\\\n    <b>Opposing conclusion:</b> Most people believe tomatoes are vegetables.<br>\\\\n    <b>Pivot:</b> \\\\\\"But.\\\\\\"<br>\\\\n    <b>Main Conclusion:</b> Tomatoes are not vegetables. (Author\'s counter.)<br>\\\\n    <b>Intermediate Conclusion:</b> Tomatoes are fruit.<br>\\\\n    <b>Premise:</b> Tomatoes have seeds.\\\\n  </div>\\\\n\\\\n  <p>One warning. The rebuttal often uses pronouns to point back at what was just said: \\\\\\"this position\\\\\\", \\\\\\"they\\\\\\", \\\\\\"such laws\\\\\\". When you state the conclusion to yourself, replace the pronoun with what it actually refers to. We\'ll drill this in Lesson 1.10.</p>\\\\n\\\\n  <div class=\\\\\\"src\\\\\\"><b>SOURCES:</b> Main Conclusion Synopsis.pdf (S) · main_conclusion_role_merged_note.pdf (S, Rebuttal section) · Skeletal Breakdown LR_dup1.pdf (A, tomato worked example)</div>\\\\n</div>\\\\n\\\\n<!-- LESSON 1.7 -->\\\\n<div class=\\\\\\"lesson\\\\\\">\\\\n  <span class=\\\\\\"lesson-num\\\\\\">1.7</span>\\\\n  <h4>Intermediate conclusions vs multi-premise arguments</h4>\\\\n  <p class=\\\\\\"hook\\\\\\">Two premises and one conclusion is simple. Add an intermediate conclusion and the chain gets longer. Confusing them is one of the test\'s favorite tricks.</p>\\\\n\\\\n  <p>Up to now, every argument we\'ve discussed has been: premises → conclusion. But arguments can stack. The author can support a claim with a sub-claim that has its own evidence underneath it. That middle layer is called the <em class=\\\\\\"named\\\\\\">intermediate conclusion</em>: it is a statement that the premises support, which itself supports the main conclusion.</p>\\\\n\\\\n  <p>Students confuse this with a multi-premise argument. The difference is whether the supporting claims connect to each other or operate independently. In a multi-premise argument, two or more premises each independently push toward the conclusion. In an intermediate-conclusion argument, the premise supports a sub-claim, and the sub-claim supports the main conclusion.</p>\\",\\"numLines\\":300,\\"startLine\\":1110,\\"totalLines\\":4613}},\\"sourceToolAssistantUUID\\":\\"717575ba-c460-493a-94c8-0a2ea5bf24f2\\",\\"userType\\":\\"external\\",\\"entrypoint\\":\\"claude-desktop\\",\\"cwd\\":\\"/Users/joshuaf/Documents/Claude/02_PROJECTS/main-conclusion-bootcamp\\",\\"sessionId\\":\\"67727c4f-fb40-4178-adfe-0d11de3fd00d\\",\\"version\\":\\"2.1.121\\",\\"gitBranch\\":\\"HEAD\\",\\"slug\\":\\"read-main-conclusion-handoff-md-http-han-staged-mountain\\"}\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"3f6445","wall_time_seconds":0.5786436,"exit_code":1,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

Every accepted response follows a\\n// durable database commit; raw provider payloads and Booker PII never enter logs.\\nimport { createClient } from \\"npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2\\";\\nimport Stripe from \\"npm:stripe@18.5.0\\";\\n\\nimport { insertEvent, listEvents, meetUrlFromEvent } from \\"../_shared/googleCalendar.ts\\";\\nimport { hasConflictingCalendarEvent, SINGLE_SESSION, validateSingleSessionCheckout } from \\"../_shared/singleSessionCore.ts\\";\\nimport { encryptEnvelope, generateCapabilityToken, hmacHex, sha256Hex } from \\"../_shared/singleSessionCrypto.ts\\";\\nimport {\\n  listPaymentLinkLineItems,\\n  retrievePaymentLink,\\n} from \\"../_shared/stripe.ts\\";\\nimport { recordCharge, stripeGet } from \\"../_shared/stripeLedger.ts\\";\\n\\nconst MAX_BODY_BYTES = 256 * 1024;\\nconst APP_ORIGIN = Deno.env.get(\\"APP_ORIGIN\\") || \\"https://lsatprep.study\\";\\n\\nconst json = (body: unknown, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {\\n  status,\\n  headers: { \\"Content-Type\\": \\"application/json\\", \\"Cache-Control\\": \\"no-store\\" },\\n});\\n\\nfunction requiredEnv(name: string): string {\\n  const value = Deno.env.get(name);\\n  if (!value) throw new Error(`${name.toLowerCase()}_not_configured`);\\n  return value;\\n}\\n\\nfunction expectedLivemode(): boolean {\\n  const value = requiredEnv(\\"STRIPE_LIVEMODE\\").toLowerCase();\\n  if (value !== \\"true\\" && value !== \\"false\\") throw new Error(\\"stripe_livemode_invalid\\");\\n  return value === \\"true\\";\\n}\\n\\nasync function readRaw(req: Request): Promise<string> {\\n  const declared = Number(req.headers.get(\\"content-length\\") || \\"0\\");\\n  if (declared > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error(\\"payload_too_large\\");\\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await req.arrayBuffer());\\n  if (bytes.byteLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) throw new Error(\\"payload_too_large\\");\\n  return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);\\n}\\n\\nfunction safeEqual(a: string, b: string) {\\n  if (a.length !== b.length) return false;\\n  let difference = 0;\\n  for (let index = 0; index < a.length; index++) difference |= a.charCodeAt(index) ^ b.charCodeAt(index);\\n  return difference === 0;\\n}\\n\\nasync function verifyCal(raw: string, signature: string | null) {\\n  const secret = requiredEnv(\\"CALCOM_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET\\");\\n  return !!signature && safeEqual(await hmacHex(secret, raw), signature.replace(/^sha256=/i, \\"\\"));\\n}\\n\\nasync function matchStudent(supabase: any, email: string | null, name: string | null) {\\n  if (email) {\\n    const wanted = email.trim().toLowerCase();\\n    let page = 1;\\n    while (page <= 5) {\\n      const { data, error } = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers({ page, perPage: 200 });\\n      if (error) throw error;\\n      const users = data?.users ?? [];\\n      const hit = users.find((user: any) => (user.email ?? \\"\\").trim().toLowerCase() === wanted);\\n      if (hit) {\\n        const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await supabase\\n          .from(\\"profiles\\").select(\\"class_id,display_name\\").eq(\\"class_id\\", hit.id).maybeSingle();\\n        if (profileError) throw profileError;\\n        if (profile) return { id: profile.class_id, display_name: profile.display_name, email: hit.email };\\n      }\\n      if (users.length < 200) break;\\n      page++;\\n    }\\n  }\\n  if (name) {\\n    const normalized = name.trim().replace(/\\\\s+/g, \\" \\");\\n    const { data, error } = await supabase\\n      .from(\\"profiles\\").select(\\"class_id,display_name\\").ilike(\\"display_name\\", normalized).limit(2);\\n    if (error) throw error;\\n    if ((data ?? []).length === 1) return { id: data[0].class_id, display_name: data[0].display_name, email };\\n  }\\n  return null;\\n}\\n\\nasync function calendarMirror(supabase: any, booking: { id: string; email: string | null; name: string | null; start: string; end: string }) {\\n  const { data: state, error: stateError } = await supabase.from(\\"gcal_sync_state\\").select(\\"calendar_id\\").eq(\\"id\\", 1).maybeSingle();\\n  if (stateError) throw stateError;\\n  const calendarId = state?.calendar_id || \\"primary\\";\\n  const event = await insertEvent(calendarId, {\\n    summary: `${(booking.name || booking.email || \\"Student\\").trim().split(/\\\\s+/)[0]} session`,\\n    description: \\"Booked through Cal.com and mirrored by LSAT U.\\",\\n    start: { dateTime: booking.start },\\n    end: { dateTime: booking.end },\\n    attendees: booking.email ? [{ email: booking.email }] : undefined,\\n    extendedProperties: { private: { lsatu_calcom_booking_id: booking.id } },\\n  }, { conferenceDataVersion: 1, sendUpdates: booking.email ? \\"all\\" : \\"none\\" });\\n  const { data: mirrored, error: mirrorError } = await supabase.from(\\"schedule_events\\").upsert({\\n    google_event_id: event.id,\\n    calendar_id: calendarId,\\n    title: event.summary,\\n    description: event.description,\\n    start_at: booking.start,\\n    end_at: booking.end,\\n    all_day: false,\\n    status: event.status ?? \\"confirmed\\",\\n    html_link: event.htmlLink ?? null,\\n    meet_url: meetUrlFromEvent(event),\\n    source: \\"calcom\\",\\n    etag: event.etag ?? null,\\n    last_synced_at: new Date().toISOString(),\\n    deleted_at: null,\\n  }, { onConflict: \\"google_event_id\\" }).select(\\"id\\").maybeSingle();\\n  if (mirrorError) throw mirrorError;\\n  return { googleEventId: event.id as string, scheduleEventId: mirrored?.id ?? null };\\n}\\n\\nasync function liveCalendarClear(supabase: any, booking: any): Promise<boolean> {\\n  if ([\\"booked\\", \\"refund_pending\\", \\"refunded\\", \\"refund_failed\\"].includes(booking.status)) return true;\\n  const { data: state, error } = await supabase.from(\\"gcal_sync_state\\")\\n    .select(\\"calendar_id,busy_calendar_ids\\").eq(\\"id\\", 1).maybeSingle();\\n  if (error) throw error;\\n  const ids = [...new Set([state?.calendar_id || \\"primary\\", ...(state?.busy_calendar_ids ?? [])])];\\n  for (const calendarId of ids) {\\n    const page = await listEvents(calendarId, {\\n      timeMin: booking.start_at,\\n      timeMax: booking.end_at,\\n      singleEvents: \\"true\\",\\n      showDeleted: \\"false\\",\\n      maxResults: \\"50\\",\\n    });\\n    if (hasConflictingCalendarEvent(\\n      page?.items ?? [],\\n      booking.start_at,\\n      booking.end_at,\\n      booking.gcal_event_id,\\n      booking.id,\\n    )) return false;\\n  }\\n  return true;\\n}\\n\\nasync function expectedPaymentLinkFacts(stripeSecret: string, paymentLinkId: string) {\\n  const link = await retrievePaymentLink(stripeSecret, paymentLinkId);\\n  if (!link.active) throw new Error(\\"inactive_payment_link\\");\\n  const lineItems = await listPaymentLinkLineItems(stripeSecret, paymentLinkId);\\n  if (!lineItems.length) throw new Error(\\"payment_link_has_no_items\\");\\n  let currency: string | null = null;\\n  let amount = 0;\\n  for (const item of lineItems) {\\n    const price = item.price;\\n    if (!price || price.unit_amount == null || !item.quantity) throw new Error(\\"unsupported_payment_link_price\\");\\n    currency ??= price.currency;\\n    if (currency !== price.currency) throw new Error(\\"mixed_payment_link_currency\\");\\n    amount += price.unit_amount * item.quantity;\\n  }\\n  return { amount, currency };\\n}\\n\\n// Server-to-server nudge so a paid booking\'s calendar promotion and emails run\\n// immediately instead of waiting for the scheduled worker. The worker secret is\\n// read from the function environment and never logged or returned. Failures are\\n// swallowed: the every-minute scheduled worker is the retry safety net, and a\\n// paid booking must never be undone by a missed nudge.\\nasync function kickOutboxWorker(): Promise<void> {\\n  const secret = Deno.env.get(\\"BOOKING_WORKER_SECRET\\");\\n  if (!secret) {\\n    console.error(\\"outbox nudge skipped: worker secret not configured\\");\\n    return;\\n  }\\n  try {\\n    const res = await fetch(`${requiredEnv(\\"SUPABASE_URL\\")}/functions/v1/single-session-outbox`, {\\n      method: \\"POST\\",\\n      headers: { \\"Content-Type\\": \\"application/json\\", \\"x-worker-secret\\": secret },\\n      body: \\"{}\\",\\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(10_000),\\n    });\\n    if (!res.ok) console.error(\\"outbox nudge non-2xx\\", { status: res.status });\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    console.error(\\"outbox nudge failed\\", { error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) });\\n  }\\n}\\n\\nasync function handleStripe(supabase: any, raw: string, signature: string | null) {\\n  const stripeSecret = requiredEnv(\\"STRIPE_SECRET_KEY\\");\\n  if (!signature) return json({ error: \\"invalid_signature\\" }, 400);\\n  const stripe = new Stripe(stripeSecret, {\\n    apiVersion: \\"2025-08-27.basil\\",\\n    httpClient: Stripe.createFetchHttpClient(),\\n  });\\n  let event: Stripe.Event;\\n  try {\\n    event = await stripe.webhooks.constructEventAsync(\\n      raw,\\n      signature,\\n      requiredEnv(\\"STRIPE_BOOKING_WEBHOOK_SECRET\\"),\\n      300,\\n    );\\n  } catch (_) {\\n    return json({ error: \\"invalid_signature\\" }, 400);\\n  }\\n\\n  // Every charge lands in the teacher\'s payments ledger. The ledger is a view,\\n  // never a trigger, so a failure here is logged and never makes Stripe retry.\\n  const ledgerChargeId = await chargeIdForLedger(event, stripeSecret).catch(() => null);\\n  if (ledgerChargeId) {\\n    try {\\n      await recordCharge(supabase, stripeSecret, ledgerChargeId, \\"webhook\\");\\n    } catch (err) {\\n      console.error(\\"ledger write failed\\", { event: event.id, type: event.type, error: err instanceof Error ? err.message : String(err) });\\n    }\\n    if (event.type.startsWith(\\"charge.\\")) return json({ received: true });\\n  }\\n\\n  if (event.type.startsWith(\\"refund.\\")) {\\n    const refund = event.data.object as Stripe.Refund;\\n    if (event.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: \\"livemode_mismatch\\" }, 400);\\n    const bookingId = String(refund.metadata?.booking_id || \\"\\");\\n    if (!bookingId || !refund.id || !refund.status) return json({ error: \\"invalid_refund\\" }, 400);\\n    const { error } = await supabase.rpc(\\"record_single_session_refund_status\\", {\\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\\n      _stripe_refund_id: refund.id,\\n      _provider_status: refund.status,\\n    });\\n    if (error) throw error;\\n    return json({ received: true });\\n  }\\n\\n  if (event.type === \\"checkout.session.expired\\") {\\n    const session = event.data.object;\\n    if (session.metadata?.product !== \\"single_session\\") return json({ received: true, ignored: \\"not_single_session\\" });\\n    if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: \\"livemode_mismatch\\" }, 400);\\n    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || \\"\\");\\n    if (!bookingId || !session.id) return json({ error: \\"invalid_session\\" }, 400);\\n    const { error } = await supabase.rpc(\\"expire_single_session_checkout\\", {\\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\\n      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,\\n      _reason: \\"stripe_checkout_expired\\",\\n    });\\n    if (error && error.code !== \\"P0409\\") throw error;\\n    return json({ received: true });\\n  }\\n\\n  if (event.type !== \\"checkout.session.completed\\") return json({ received: true });\\n  const session = event.data.object;\\n\\n  // A payment never changes access. Access is Joshua\'s decision, made by tier\\n  // on the People page, and it never changes by itself. What a payment does is\\n  // tell him, so a paying student is not left waiting unnoticed in the queue.\\n  if (session.payment_status === \\"paid\\") {\\n    try {\\n      await notifyAdminsOfPayment(supabase, {\\n        email: session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null,\\n        name: session.customer_details?.name ?? null,\\n        amountCents: typeof session.amount_total === \\"number\\" ? session.amount_total : null,\\n        currency: session.currency ?? null,\\n      });\\n    } catch (err) {\\n      // A notice is a convenience on top of the payment record. Failing to\\n      // write it must never make Stripe retry a payment that was recorded.\\n      console.error(\\"payment notice failed\\", { event: event.id, err });\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  if (session.metadata?.product === \\"single_session\\") {\\n    const bookingId = String(session.metadata?.booking_id || \\"\\");\\n    if (!bookingId) return json({ error: \\"invalid_product\\" }, 400);\\n    if (!session.id || typeof session.payment_intent !== \\"string\\") return json({ error: \\"missing_payment_facts\\" }, 400);\\n    const { data: booking, error: bookingError } = await supabase.from(\\"single_session_bookings\\")\\n      .select(\\"id,status,start_at,end_at,gcal_event_id,event_slug,email,quoted_amount_cents,quoted_currency\\")\\n      .eq(\\"id\\", bookingId).maybeSingle();\\n    if (bookingError) throw bookingError;\\n    if (!booking) return json({ error: \\"unknown_booking\\" }, 400);\\n    const { data: bookingEvent, error: eventError } = await supabase.from(\\"booking_event_types\\")\\n      .select(\\"price_cents,currency\\")\\n      .eq(\\"slug\\", booking.event_slug).maybeSingle();\\n    if (eventError) throw eventError;\\n    if (!bookingEvent) return json({ error: \\"unknown_event\\" }, 400);\\n    // New holds carry the exact quote used to create Checkout. Rows created\\n    // before quote persistence use the same override-aware resolver as checkout.\\n    let expectedAmount = Number(booking.quoted_amount_cents);\\n    if (!Number.isInteger(booking.quoted_amount_cents)) {\\n      const { data: legacyAmount, error: legacyPriceError } = await supabase.rpc(\\"booking_price_cents\\", {\\n        _event_slug: booking.event_slug,\\n        _email: booking.email,\\n      });\\n      if (legacyPriceError) throw legacyPriceError;\\n      expectedAmount = Number(legacyAmount ?? bookingEvent.price_cents);\\n    }\\n    const expectedCurrency = String(booking.quoted_currency || bookingEvent.currency).toLowerCase();\\n    const validation = validateSingleSessionCheckout(\\n      session,\\n      expectedLivemode(),\\n      expectedAmount,\\n      expectedCurrency,\\n    );\\n    if (!validation.ok) return json({ error: validation.error }, 400);\\n\\n    const cancelToken = generateCapabilityToken();\\n    const encryptedCancelPayload = await encryptEnvelope({\\n      cancel_token: cancelToken,\\n      cancel_url: `${APP_ORIGIN}/book/session?cancel_token=${encodeURIComponent(cancelToken)}`,\\n    }, requiredEnv(\\"SINGLE_SESSION_ENCRYPTION_KEY\\"));\\n    const { data: result, error } = await supabase.rpc(\\"finalize_single_session_payment\\", {\\n      _booking_id: bookingId,\\n      _provider_event_id: event.id,\\n      _stripe_checkout_session_id: session.id,\\n      _stripe_payment_intent_id: session.payment_intent,\\n      _amount_cents: session.amount_total,\\n      _currency: session.currency,\\n      _livemode: session.livemode,\\n      _live_calendar_clear: await liveCalendarClear(supabase, booking),\\n      _cancel_token_hash: await sha256Hex(cancelToken),\\n      _cancel_payload_ciphertext: encryptedCancelPayload,\\n    });\\n    if (error) throw error;\\n    // Keep the Stripe response fast: the nudge runs after the response is sent\\n    // when the runtime supports it, and is otherwise best-effort.\\n    const nudge = kickOutboxWorker();\\n    const waitUntil = (globalThis as any)?.EdgeRuntime?.waitUntil;\\n    if (typeof waitUntil === \\"function\\") waitUntil.call((globalThis as any).EdgeRuntime, nudge);\\n    else void nudge;\\n    return json({ received: true, booking_id: result?.booking_id, status: result?.status });\\n  }\\n\\n  // Existing package branch, now protected by immutable payment validation.\\n  if (session.mode !== \\"payment\\" || session.payment_status !== \\"paid\\") return json({ error: \\"payment_not_complete\\" }, 400);\\n  if (session.livemode !== expectedLivemode()) return json({ error: \\"livemode_mismatch\\" }, 400);\\n  const paymentLink = typeof session.payment_link === \\"string\\" ? session.payment_link : session.payment_link?.id;\\n  if (!paymentLink) return json({ received: true, ignored: \\"not_booking_commerce\\" });\\n  const { data: offer, error: offerError } = await supabase.from(\\"booking_offers\\")\\n    .select(\\"id,credits\\").eq(\\"provider\\", \\"stripe\\").eq(\\"provider_ref\\", paymentLink).eq(\\"active\\", true).maybeSingle();\\n  if (offerError) throw offerError;\\n  if (!offer) return json({ received: true, ignored: \\"unknown_payment_link\\" });\\n  const expected = await expectedPaymentLinkFacts(stripeSecret, paymentLink);\\n  if (session.amount_total !== expected.amount) return json({ error: \\"amount_mismatch\\" }, 400);\\n  if (String(session.currency || \\"\\").toLowerCase() !== expected.currency) return json({ error: \\"currency_mismatch\\" }, 400);\\n  const email = session.customer_details?.email ?? session.customer_email ?? null;\\n  const student = await matchStudent(supabase, email, null);\\n  if (!student) {\\n    const { error } = await supabase.from(\\"booking_commerce_events\\").upsert({\\n      provider: \\"stripe\\",\\n      provider_event_id: event.id,\\n      event_type: \\"unmatched_paid\\",\\n      offer_id: offer.id,\\n      email,\\n      amount_cents: session.amount_total,\\n      currency: session.currency,\\n      payload: { checkout_session_id: session.id, payment_link: paymentLink, payment_status:
