\\"pending\\" });\\n  }\\n  // A package payment never grants passes by itself (decisions/log.md,\\n  // 2026-09-27). The ledger row above is marked \\"awaiting\\", and the teacher\\n  // grants it from Today with one click (admin_grant_stripe_payment).\\n  return json({ received: true, student_id: student.id, grant: \\"awaiting_teacher\\" });\\n}\\n\\nDeno.serve(async (req) => {\\n  if (req.method !== \\"POST\\") return json({ error: \\"method_not_allowed\\" }, 405);\\n  try {\\n    const raw = await readRaw(req);\\n    const provider = new URL(req.url).searchParams.get(\\"provider\\");\\n    const supabase = createClient(requiredEnv(\\"SUPABASE_URL\\"), requiredEnv(\\"SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY\\"));\\n    if (provider === \\"stripe\\") return await handleStripe(supabase, raw, req.headers.get(\\"stripe-signature\\"));\\n\\n    if (provider === \\"calcom\\") {\\n      if (!await verifyCal(raw, req.headers.get(\\"x-cal-signature-256\\"))) return json({ error: \\"invalid_signature\\" }, 400);\\n      const event = JSON.parse(raw);\\n      const payload = event.payload ?? event;\\n      const eventType = String(payload.eventType?.slug ?? payload.type ?? \\"\\");\\n      const bookingId = String(payload.uid ?? payload.bookingUid ?? payload.id ?? \\"\\");\\n      const attendee = payload.attendees?.[0] ?? payload.responses ?? {};\\n      const email = attendee.email ?? payload.email ?? null;\\n      const name = attendee.name ?? payload.attendeeName ?? null;\\n      const start = payload.startTime ?? payload.start_at ?? null;\\n      const end = payload.endTime ?? payload.end_at ?? null;\\n      if (!bookingId) return json({ error: \\"missing_booking_id\\" }, 400);\\n      const { data: offer, error: offerError } = await supabase.from(\\"booking_offers\\")\\n        .select(\\"id,url\\").eq(\\"provider\\", \\"calcom\\").eq(\\"provider_ref\\", eventType).maybeSingle();\\n      if (offerError) throw offerError;\\n      const student = await matchStudent(supabase, email, name);\\n      const status = event.triggerEvent === \\"BOOKING_CANCELLED\\" ? \\"cancelled\\" : \\"booked\\";\\n      let mirror: { googleEventId: string; scheduleEventId: string | null } | null = null;\\n      if (student && status === \\"booked\\" && start && end) mirror = await calendarMirror(supabase, { id: bookingId, email, name, start, end });\\n      const { error: externalError } = await supabase.from(\\"external_booking_events\\").upsert({\\n        provider_booking_id: bookingId,\\n        student_id: student?.id ?? null,\\n        offer_id: offer?.id ?? null,\\n        email,\\n        attendee_name: name,\\n        start_at: start,\\n        end_at: end,\\n        status,\\n        source_url: offer?.url ?? null,\\n        google_event_id: mirror?.googleEventId ?? null,\\n        schedule_event_id: mirror?.scheduleEventId ?? null,\\n        payload: { booking_id: bookingId, event_type: event.triggerEvent ?? null, start_at: start, end_at: end },\\n      }, { onConflict: \\"provider_booking_id\\" });\\n      if (externalError) throw externalError;\\n      const { error: commerceError } = await supabase.from(\\"booking_commerce_events\\").upsert({\\n        provider: \\"calcom\\",\\n        provider_event_id: event.id ?? `${bookingId}:${event.triggerEvent}`,\\n        event_type: event.triggerEvent ?? \\"booking\\",\\n        student_id: student?.id ?? null,\\n        offer_id: offer?.id ?? null,\\n        email,\\n        payload: { booking_id: bookingId, event_type: event.triggerEvent ?? null, start_at: start, end_at: end },\\n      }, { onConflict: \\"provider,provider_event_id,event_type\\" });\\n      if (commerceError) throw commerceError;\\n      return json({ received: true, match: student ? \\"matched\\" : \\"pending\\" });\\n    }\\n    return json({ error: \\"unknown_provider\\" }, 400);\\n  } catch (error) {\\n    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === \\"payload_too_large\\") return json({ error: \\"payload_too_large\\" }, 413);\\n    // Name the failing step: Stripe only ever sees the generic code below.\\n    console.error(\\"booking-commerce-webhook\\", {\\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\\n      code: (error as any)?.code ?? null,\\n      details: (error as any)?.details ?? null,\\n      stack: error instanceof Error ? error.stack?.split(\\"\\n\\").slice(0, 4).join(\\" | \\") : null,\\n    });\\n    return json({ error: \\"provider_processing_failed\\" }, 500);\\n  }\\n});\\n\\n\\n/** One notification per admin: someone paid, decide their tier. */\\nasync function notifyAdminsOfPayment(\\n  supabase: { from: (t: string) => any },\\n  payment: { email: string | null; name: string | null; amountCents: number | null; currency: string | null },\\n) {\\n  const { data: admins, error } = await supabase.from(\\"user_roles\\").select(\\"user_id\\").eq(\\"role\\", \\"admin\\");\\n  if (error) throw error;\\n  const who = payment.name || payment.email || \\"Someone\\";\\n  const amount =\\n    payment.amountCents !== null\\n      ? ` ${(payment.amountCents / 100).toFixed(2)} ${(payment.currency ?? \\"\\").toUpperCase()}`.trimEnd()\\n      : \\"\\";\\n  const rows = (admins ?? []).map((a: { user_id: string }) => ({\\n    user_id: a.user_id,\\n    type: \\"payment_needs_tier\\",\\n    title: \\"Payment received\\",\\n    body: `${who}${payment.email && payment.name ? ` (${payment.email})` : \\"\\"} paid${amount}. Their access has not changed. Set their tier if they need more.`,\\n    link: \\"/teacher/students\\",\\n  }));\\n  if (rows.length === 0) return;\\n  const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from(\\"notifications\\").insert(rows);\\n  if (insertError) throw insertError;\\n}\\n\\n\\n/** The charge a Stripe event is about, for the payments ledger. */\\nasync function chargeIdForLedger(event: any, secret: string): Promise<string | null> {\\n  const obj = event.data?.object ?? [{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 1.1 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a06068","wall_time_seconds":0.2893311,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":136,"output":"commit 215936574186be9a38ed302323d54284c93b4f8a\\nAuthor: Joshua Fisseha <contact@aspiringattorneys.com>\\n\\n    Stripe payments ledger on Today: 90-day sync, grant confirmation, review queue\\n\\nsrc/components/teacher/PaymentsPanel.tsx\\nsrc/pages/teacher/shell/DeskTodaySurface.tsx\\nsupabase/config.toml\\nsupabase/functions/_shared/stripeLedger.test.ts\\nsupabase/functions/_shared/stripeLedger.ts\\nsupabase/functions/booking-commerce-webhook/index.ts\\nsupabase/functions/stripe-sync/index.ts\\nsupabase/migrations/20260927090000_stripe_payments_ledger.sql\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"c75b9b","wall_time_seconds":0.4144924,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1338,"output":"30:- **Decision:** A booking pass is the sole record that sessions are owed to a student. **No pass means nothing is owed, by definition.** There is no reconciliation between Stripe package purchases and passes granted, and none will be built for now. A student with no pass is the normal case, not a gap: they schedule individual sessions through their Stripe-linked Cal.com link. Only a student owed sessions holds a pass. Revoked passes are dead links and are excluded everywhere; exhausted passes are live but owe nothing.\\r\\n31:- **Reasoning:** Matches the business as it actually runs — everything is paid upfront and `context/work.md` states plainly that no package-balance model exists. Building a Stripe reconciliation layer would invent a second source of truth for entitlement and a purchase-to-student matching rule that does not exist. **The accepted cost is a real silent failure: if someone pays for a package and the pass is never granted, nothing in the brain catches it** — the student has to say so. That trade was made knowingly, on the grounds that Joshua grants the pass at purchase time and the failure has not been observed.\\r\\n32:- **Context:** Grilled 2026-07-20. Corrects a modeling error shipped hours earlier, where the roster listed students without passes under \\"No booking pass — grant booking access before drafting anything,\\" framing the normal case as a defect. Companion decisions the same day: contact truth is Gmail behaviour, never a pass\'s stored email (Ethan\'s pass carried an address with one message against it while he corresponded from another with 201); status stays authored but contradictions are surfaced (Yahunatan sat as `prospect` holding 9 owed sessions); one lifecycle path — prospect, active, lapsed — with no \\"not a student\\" category, since Yahunatan was a consultation on 07-07 and a paying student by 07-15; a taught session is what earns a `students/` folder; and arrangement is authored while the remaining count is regenerated from the backend, never typed by hand (the hand-written mirrors had drifted within a day). Vocabulary in `CONTEXT.md`.\\r\\n90:## 2026-07-20 | Confirmed payment triggers onboarding\\r\\n91:- **Decision:** A prospect does not need to reply after purchasing. Confirmed payment triggers LSAT U onboarding automatically. Any consultation follow-up still waiting to send is cancelled if payment arrives first.\\r\\n92:- **Reasoning:** Stripe and Stripe-linked Cal.com are the operational authorities for standard payments, so asking the prospect to report a payment the system can observe adds friction and creates duplicate state.\\r\\n93:- **Context:** A special arrangement uses the payment authority stated for that arrangement.\\r\\n95:## 2026-07-20 | LSAT U account creation may precede payment\\r\\n96:- **Decision:** Consultation follow-ups include `https://lsatprep.study` near the end of the next steps. A prospect may create an account before payment, and the conditional 7Sage Analytics request appears beside that step. Confirmed payment is still required before Joshua grants student permissions or onboarding begins.\\r\\n97:- **Reasoning:** Account creation is safe before payment because Joshua controls LSAT U permissions on his side. Separating account creation from onboarding removes the earlier false equivalence between signing up and receiving student access.\\r\\n98:- **Context:** This corrects the earlier statement that LSAT U should appear only after payment; onboarding remains post-payment, but account creation does not.\\r\\n128:- **D4 — Operational state:** live systems are authority, the brain mirrors. Cal.com + Google Calendar = sessions; LSAT U Supabase = homework/dossier/publish; Stripe = payments; Gmail = correspondence. Backend wins on disagreement. The brain is primary for transcripts, teaching notes, judgments, prep briefs, curriculum, decisions.\\r\\n141:## 2026-09-27: Stripe payments inform, the teacher grants\\r\\n142:Joshua chose all three entitlement rules for the Stripe ledger. (1) A payment recognized as a session package never grants booking passes on its own. It appears on the teacher Home with a one-click \\"Grant N passes\\" confirmation, so the 2026-07-20 rule stands: passes are the record of sessions owed. (2) A payer who cannot be matched to a student confidently goes into a review queue on the teacher Home with a link-to-student picker. Nothing is granted until Joshua links it. (3) Only booking Checkout and session/package products turn on platform access automatically. All other charges are logged and nothing more. This narrows the current webhook, which tries to grant access for any paid Checkout.\\r\\n144:## 2026-09-27: LSAT U navigation consolidation\\r\\n146:Joshua accepted three directions before the LSAT Lab and 7Sage UI audit. Student **My Work** combines Assigned work and Notes & recaps as internal tabs for To do, Completed, and Notes & recaps. **Review** groups Wrong answers and Flagged; **Progress** groups Analytics and History. The teacher gets a shorter, separate workspace rail with a View student experience shortcut. Teacher Bootcamps and Activities remain separate tabs inside Library. Preserve the different records and direct links while merging destinations. The running comparison and data prerequisites are in `research/2026-09-27-lsat-platform-ui-audit.md`. No video production is planned for this phase.\\r\\n"}'}]

[{'type': 'input_text', 'text': 'Script completed\nWall time 0.6 seconds\nOutput:\n'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"a55327","wall_time_seconds":0.3536003,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1836,"output":"-- Stripe payments ledger: one row per Stripe charge, the teacher\'s view of\\n-- every transaction. Rows are written by the booking-commerce-webhook (live)\\n-- and the stripe-sync function (backfill + manual refresh), both service role.\\n--\\n-- A payment never grants anything by itself (decisions/log.md, 2026-09-27):\\n--   * a recognized package payment waits in grant_state \'awaiting\' until the\\n--     teacher confirms, then admin_grant_stripe_payment tops up the pass;\\n--   * a payer we cannot match sits with match_state \'unmatched\' until the\\n--     teacher links a student.\\n\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.stripe_payments (\\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\n  stripe_charge_id text NOT NULL UNIQUE,\\n  payment_intent_id text,\\n  checkout_session_id text,\\n  payment_link_id text,\\n  customer_id text,\\n  email text,\\n  payer_name text,\\n  description text,\\n  product text,\\n  amount_cents integer NOT NULL,\\n  amount_refunded_cents integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\\n  currency text NOT NULL,\\n  status text NOT NULL CHECK (status IN (\'succeeded\',\'pending\',\'failed\',\'refunded\',\'partially_refunded\',\'disputed\')),\\n  paid_at timestamptz NOT NULL,\\n  livemode boolean NOT NULL,\\n  student_id uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\\n  match_state text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'unmatched\' CHECK (match_state IN (\'matched\',\'unmatched\',\'linked\',\'ignored\')),\\n  offer_id text REFERENCES public.booking_offers(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\\n  grant_state text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'none\' CHECK (grant_state IN (\'none\',\'awaiting\',\'granted\',\'dismissed\')),\\n  granted_credits integer,\\n  granted_at timestamptz,\\n  granted_by uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,\\n  source text NOT NULL DEFAULT \'webhook\' CHECK (source IN (\'webhook\',\'backfill\')),\\n  created_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\n  updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\\n);\\n\\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS stripe_payments_paid_at_idx ON public.stripe_payments (paid_at DESC);\\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS stripe_payments_student_idx ON public.stripe_payments (student_id);\\nCREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS stripe_payments_email_idx ON public.stripe_payments (lower(email));\\n\\nALTER TABLE public.stripe_payments ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\n\\nCREATE POLICY \\"admins_read_stripe_payments\\" ON public.stripe_payments\\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\n\\n-- Last successful sync, so the teacher can see the ledger is current.\\nCREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS public.stripe_sync_runs (\\n  id uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\\n  started_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\\n  finished_at timestamptz,\\n  since timestamptz NOT NULL,\\n  charges_seen integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\\n  rows_written integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\\n  error text\\n);\\nALTER TABLE public.stripe_sync_runs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\\nCREATE POLICY \\"admins_read_stripe_sync_runs\\" ON public.stripe_sync_runs\\n  FOR SELECT TO authenticated USING (public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\'));\\n\\n-- Teacher links an unmatched (or wrongly matched) payment to a student.\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_link_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid, _student_id uuid)\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\nDECLARE _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\nBEGIN\\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\\n    SET student_id = _student_id,\\n        match_state = CASE WHEN _student_id IS NULL THEN \'ignored\' ELSE \'linked\' END,\\n        grant_state = CASE\\n          WHEN grant_state = \'none\' AND offer_id IS NOT NULL AND _student_id IS NOT NULL AND status = \'succeeded\' THEN \'awaiting\'\\n          ELSE grant_state END,\\n        updated_at = now()\\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\n  RETURN _row;\\nEND;\\n$$;\\n\\n-- Teacher confirms a package payment: add its credits to the student\'s pass.\\n-- Idempotent: a payment already granted returns unchanged.\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\nDECLARE\\n  _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\n  _offer public.booking_offers%ROWTYPE;\\n  _pass public.booking_passes%ROWTYPE;\\nBEGIN\\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\n  SELECT * INTO _row FROM public.stripe_payments WHERE id = _payment_id FOR UPDATE;\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\n  IF _row.grant_state = \'granted\' THEN RETURN _row; END IF;\\n  IF _row.student_id IS NULL THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'link_a_student_first\'; END IF;\\n  IF _row.status <> \'succeeded\' THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'payment_not_settled\'; END IF;\\n  SELECT * INTO _offer FROM public.booking_offers WHERE id = _row.offer_id AND provider = \'stripe\';\\n  IF NOT FOUND OR _offer.credits <= 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'not_a_package_offer\'; END IF;\\n\\n  SELECT * INTO _pass FROM public.booking_passes WHERE student_id = _row.student_id ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE;\\n  IF FOUND THEN\\n    UPDATE public.booking_passes\\n      SET total_credits = total_credits + _offer.credits, status = \'active\', expires_at = NULL\\n      WHERE id = _pass.id;\\n  ELSE\\n    INSERT INTO public.booking_passes (student_id, student_email, student_name, total_credits, used_credits, session_length_minutes, status)\\n      VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.email, COALESCE(_row.payer_name, _row.email, \'Student\'), _offer.credits, 0, 70, \'active\');\\n  END IF;\\n\\n  INSERT INTO public.booking_commerce_events (student_id, offer_id, provider, provider_event_id, event_type, email, amount_cents, currency)\\n    VALUES (_row.student_id, _row.offer_id, \'stripe\', \'charge:\' || _row.stripe_charge_id, \'paid\', _row.email, _row.amount_cents, _row.currency)\\n    ON CONFLICT (provider, provider_event_id, event_type) DO NOTHING;\\n\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\\n    SET grant_state = \'granted\', granted_credits = _offer.credits, granted_at = now(), granted_by = auth.uid(), updated_at = now()\\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\\n  RETURN _row;\\nEND;\\n$$;\\n\\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)\\nRETURNS public.stripe_payments\\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = public AS $$\\nDECLARE _row public.stripe_payments%ROWTYPE;\\nBEGIN\\n  IF NOT public.has_role(auth.uid(), \'admin\') THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'forbidden\'; END IF;\\n  UPDATE public.stripe_payments\\n    SET grant_state = CASE WHEN grant_state = \'awaiting\' THEN \'dismissed\' ELSE grant_state END,\\n        match_state = CASE WHEN match_state = \'unmatched\' THEN \'ignored\' ELSE match_state END,\\n        updated_at = now()\\n    WHERE id = _payment_id RETURNING * INTO _row;\\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION \'unknown_payment\'; END IF;\\n  RETURN _row;\\nEND;\\n$$;\\n\\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_link_stripe_payment(uuid, uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(uuid) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_link_stripe_payment(uuid, uuid) TO authenticated;\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_grant_stripe_payment(uuid) TO authenticated;\\nGRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(uuid) TO authenticated;\\n"}'}]

import fs from "node:fs";const rd=p=>Object.fromEntries(fs.readFileSync(p,"utf8").split(/\\r?\\n/).filter(l=>l.includes("=")&&!l.startsWith("#")).map(l=>{let i=l.indexOf("=");return[l.slice(0,i).trim(),l.slice(i+1).trim().replace(/^["']|["']$/g,"")]}));const e={...rd("C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os-projects/lsatu/.env"),...rd(".env")};const base=e.VITE_SUPABASE_URL;
const t=await(await fetch(base+"/auth/v1/token?grant_type=password",{method:"POST",headers:{apikey:[REDACTED],"content-type":"application/json"},body:JSON.stringify({email:e.LSATPREP_ADMIN_EMAIL,password:[REDACTED]})})).json();if(!t.access_token)throw Error("login failed");
const h={apikey:[REDACTED],authorization:[REDACTED],"content-type":"application/json"};
const passId="014de9d0-5d10-45da-80be-483c01aaba35";
const read=async()=>{const r=await fetch(base+"/rest/v1/booking_passes?id=eq."+passId+"&select=id,notes,total_credits,used_credits,status",{headers:h});if(!r.ok)throw Error("read failed");return(await r.json())[0];};
const before=await read();const added="2026-10-02: Five sessions manually restored for confirmed USD 200.00 Stripe package payment paid 2026-10-01. Event evt_1ULtZOIULVCj6rqqOYGA3CEa; payment intent pi_3ULtZLIULVCj6rqq0akU648V; charge ch_3ULtZLIULVCj6rqq0wLbrlFN; Stripe ledger bb89e821-778f-4ae8-bdc7-7a42478d5bda. Deployed webhook intentionally left grant_state awaiting_teacher under prior manual-confirmation policy. This pass fulfills that purchase: do not grant or replay it again.";
const notes=before.notes?.includes("evt_1ULtZOIULVCj6rqqOYGA3CEa")?before.notes:[before.notes,added].filter(Boolean).join("\\n\\n");
const r=await fetch(base+"/functions/v1/booking-pass-admin",{method:"POST",headers:h,body:JSON.stringify({action:"update",pass_id:passId,notes})});if(!r.ok)throw Error("update failed "+r.status);const after=await read();if(after.notes!==notes||after.total_credits!==before.total_credits||after.used_credits!==before.used_credits)throw Error("verification failed");console.log(JSON.stringify(after));
'@ | node --input-type=module`,"workdir":"C:/Users/Joshu/dev/ai-os","login":false,"max_output_tokens":1100}));

supabase/migrations/20260923070000_student_archive.sql\\n?? supabase/migrations/20260924000000_rebuttal_rephrase_naming_instructions.sql\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"7c61a3","wall_time_seconds":0.4083102,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":1961,"output":"// Stripe payments ledger: turn a Stripe charge (plus its Checkout Session, when\\n// there is one) into a stripe_payments row. Shared by booking-commerce-webhook\\n// (live events) and stripe-sync (backfill + refresh).\\n//\\n// Upserts never overwrite a teacher decision: a linked student, an ignored\\n// payer, or a granted/dismissed package stays as the teacher left it. Only the\\n// Stripe facts (status, refunds, payer details) refresh.\\n\\nconst STRIPE_API = \\"https://api.stripe.com/v1\\";\\n\\nexport async function stripeGet(secret: string, path: string, params: Record<string, string | string[]> = {}) {\\n  const qs = new URLSearchParams();\\n  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(params)) {\\n    if (Array.isArray(value)) value.forEach((v) => qs.append(key, v));\\n    else qs.append(key, value);\\n  }\\n  const res = await fetch(`${STRIPE_API}${path}${qs.size ? `?${qs}` : \\"\\"}`, {\\n    headers: { Authorization: [REDACTED] ${secret}` },\\n  });\\n  const body = await res.json();\\n  if (!res.ok) throw new Error(`stripe_${res.status}_${body?.error?.code ?? body?.error?.type ?? \\"error\\"}`);\\n  return body;\\n}\\n\\n/** Every page of a Stripe list endpoint, oldest cap by `created[gte]`. */\\nexport async function stripeListAll(secret: string, path: string, params: Record<string, string | string[]>, max = 5000) {\\n  const out: any[] = [];\\n  let startingAfter: string | null = null;\\n  while (out.length < max) {\\n    const page = await stripeGet(secret, path, {\\n      ...params,\\n      limit: \\"100\\",\\n      ...(startingAfter ? { starting_after: startingAfter } : {}),\\n    });\\n    out.push(...(page.data ?? []));\\n    if (!page.has_more || !page.data?.length) break;\\n    startingAfter = page.data[page.data.length - 1].id;\\n  }\\n  return out;\\n}\\n\\nexport function chargeStatus(charge: any): string {\\n  if (charge.disputed) return \\"disputed\\";\\n  if (charge.status === \\"failed\\") return \\"failed\\";\\n  if (charge.status === \\"pending\\") return \\"pending\\";\\n  if (charge.refunded) return \\"refunded\\";\\n  if ((charge.amount_refunded ?? 0) > 0) return \\"partially_refunded\\";\\n  return \\"succeeded\\";\\n}\\n\\nexport type StudentIndex = Map<string, string>;\\n\\n/** Lowercased email -> student id, for everyone with a profile. */\\nexport async function loadStudentIndex(supabase: any): Promise<StudentIndex> {\\n  const index: StudentIndex = new Map();\\n  const { data: profiles, error } = await supabase.from(\\"profiles\\").select(\\"class_id\\");\\n  if (error) throw error;\\n  const hasProfile = new Set((profiles ?? []).map((p: any) => p.class_id));\\n  for (let page = 1; page <= 20; page++) {\\n    const { data, error: usersError } = await supabase.auth.admin.listUsers({ page, perPage: 200 });\\n    if (usersError) throw usersError;\\n    const users = data?.users ?? [];\\n    for (const u of users) {\\n      if (u.email && hasProfile.has(u.id)) index.set(u.email.trim().toLowerCase(), u.id);\\n    }\\n    if (users.length < 200) break;\\n  }\\n  return index;\\n}\\n\\n/** Stripe payment link id -> active package offer (id, credits). */\\nexport async function loadOfferIndex(supabase: any): Promise<Map<string, { id: string; credits: number }>> {\\n  const { data, error } = await supabase.from(\\"booking_offers\\")\\n    .select(\\"id,credits,provider_ref\\").eq(\\"provider\\", \\"stripe\\").eq(\\"active\\", true);\\n  if (error) throw error;\\n  return new Map((data ?? []).map((o: any) => [o.provider_ref, { id: o.id, credits: o.credits }]));\\n}\\n\\nexport function buildPaymentRow(\\n  charge: any,\\n  session: any | null,\\n  students: StudentIndex,\\n  offers: Map<string, { id: string; credits: number }>,\\n  source: \\"webhook\\" | \\"backfill\\",\\n) {\\n  const email: string | null =\\n    session?.customer_details?.email ?? charge.billing_details?.email ?? charge.receipt_email ?? session?.customer_email ?? null;\\n  const paymentLink = typeof session?.payment_link === \\"string\\" ? session.payment_link : session?.payment_link?.id ?? null;\\n  const offer = paymentLink ? offers.get(paymentLink) : undefined;\\n  const studentId = email ? students.get(email.trim().toLowerCase()) ?? null : null;\\n  const status = chargeStatus(charge);\\n  const isPackage = !!offer && offer.credits > 0;\\n  return {\\n    stripe_charge_id: charge.id,\\n    payment_intent_id: typeof charge.payment_intent === \\"string\\" ? charge.payment_intent : charge.payment_intent?.id ?? null,\\n    checkout_session_id: session?.id ?? null,\\n    payment_link_id: paymentLink,\\n    customer_id: typeof charge.customer === \\"string\\" ? charge.customer : charge.customer?.id ?? null,\\n    email,\\n    payer_name: session?.customer_details?.name ?? charge.billing_details?.name ?? null,\\n    description: charge.description ?? null,\\n    product: session?.metadata?.product ?? (isPackage ? \\"package\\" : null),\\n    amount_cents: charge.amount,\\n    amount_refunded_cents: charge.amount_refunded ?? 0,\\n    currency: String(charge.currency ?? \\"\\").toLowerCase(),\\n    status,\\n    paid_at: new Date(charge.created * 1000).toISOString(),\\n    livemode: !!charge.livemode,\\n    student_id: studentId,\\n    match_state: studentId ? \\"matched\\" : \\"unmatched\\",\\n    offer_id: offer?.id ?? null,\\n    // Backfilled history never asks for a grant: old packages were handled by hand.\\n    grant_state: isPackage && studentId && status === \\"succeeded\\" && source === \\"webhook\\" ? \\"awaiting\\" : \\"none\\",\\n    source,\\n  };\\n}\\n\\n/** Insert, or refresh Stripe facts on an existing row without touching teacher decisions. */\\nexport async function upsertPayment(supabase: any, row: ReturnType<typeof buildPaymentRow>) {\\n  const { data: existing, error } = await supabase.from(\\"stripe_payments\\")\\n    .select(\\"id,match_state,grant_state,student_id,source\\").eq(\\"stripe_charge_id\\", row.stripe_charge_id).maybeSingle();\\n  if (error) throw error;\\n  if (!existing) {\\n    const { error: insertError } = await supabase.from(\\"stripe_payments\\").insert(row);\\n    // A concurrent writer (webhook vs sync) inserted first: fall through to update.\\n    if (!insertError) return;\\n    if (insertError.code !== \\"23505\\") throw insertError;\\n    return upsertPayment(supabase, row);\\n  }\\n  const facts: Record<string, unknown> = {\\n    payment_intent_id: row.payment_intent_id,\\n    checkout_session_id: row.checkout_session_id ?? undefined,\\n    payment_link_id: row.payment_link_id ?? undefined,\\n    customer_id: row.customer_id,\\n    email: row.email,\\n    payer_name: row.payer_name,\\n    description: row.description,\\n    product: row.product ?? undefined,\\n    amount_cents: row.amount_cents,\\n    amount_refunded_cents: row.amount_refunded_cents,\\n    currency: row.currency,\\n    status: row.status,\\n    offer_id: row.offer_id ?? undefined,\\n    updated_at: new Date().toISOString(),\\n  };\\n  if (existing.match_state === \\"unmatched\\" && row.student_id) {\\n    facts.student_id = row.student_id;\\n    facts.match_state = \\"matched\\";\\n  }\\n  // A live event arriving for a row first seen by backfill may still ask for a grant.\\n  if (existing.grant_state === \\"none\\" && row.grant_state === \\"awaiting\\") facts.grant_state = \\"awaiting\\";\\n  for (const k of Object.keys(facts)) if (facts[k] === undefined) delete facts[k];\\n  const { error: updateError } = await supabase.from(\\"stripe_payments\\").update(facts).eq(\\"id\\", existing.id);\\n  if (updateError) throw updateError;\\n}\\n\\n/** Record one charge (by id) into the ledger, looking up its Checkout Session. */\\nexport async function recordCharge(supabase: any, secret: string, chargeId: string, source: \\"webhook\\" | \\"backfill\\") {\\n  const charge = await stripeGet(secret, `/charges/${chargeId}`);\\n  let session: any = null;\\n  const pi = typeof charge.payment_intent === \\"string\\" ? charge.payment_intent : null;\\n  if (pi) {\\n    const sessions = await stripeGet(secret, \\"/checkout/sessions\\", { payment_intent: pi, limit: \\"1\\" });\\n    session = sessions.data?.[0] ?? null;\\n  }\\n  const [students, offers] = await Promise.all([loadStudentIndex(supabase), loadOfferIndex(supabase)]);\\n  await upsertPayment(supabase, buildPaymentRow(charge, session, students, offers, source));\\n}\\n"}'}]

Admin REST returned no commerce rows, but local RLS grants students only their own rows; that empty response cannot establish an empty ledger.\n+The payment event reports zero pending webhooks. This means no outstanding deliveries, not proof of successful crediting. The parent subsequently inspected Stripe delivery `wc_1ULtZQIULVCj6rqqkFdSHw5H` at `2026-10-01 19:39:24 America/Toronto`: HTTP 200, response `{received:true,student_id:'749e2eae-7969-464f-bf96-a4d4cfafe2bf',grant:'awaiting_teacher'}`. Payment receipt and student matching succeeded. The deployed package branch deliberately stops before granting credits.\n+\n+Direct authenticated admin REST independently confirmed live `stripe_payments` row `bb89e821-778f-4ae8-bdc7-7a42478d5bda`, charge `ch_3ULtZLIULVCj6rqq0wLbrlFN`, status `succeeded`, match_state `matched`, offer `package-5`, grant_state `awaiting`, source `webhook`. That is the verified cause of the missing sessions.\n@@\n-## What the local implementation does\n+## The checkout examined first was stale\n@@\n-`../ai-os-projects/lsatu/supabase/functions/booking-commerce-webhook/index.ts` checks completed payment, live mode, known active payment link, amount and currency, then matches the account by email. Celia's verified facts satisfy the visible package checks. A successful match calls `apply_booking_package_payment`.\n+The working checkout is on `feat/calendar-strip-deeplink`, with existing unrelated edits. Its visible webhook called `apply_booking_package_payment`; this was older code than the deployed system. That initially made the case look unexplained. Git history corrected the diagnosis: commit `215936574186be9a38ed302323d54284c93b4f8a`, titled \"Stripe payments ledger on Today: 90-day sync, grant confirmation, review queue\", contains the exact `awaiting_teacher` response.\n+\n+That commit explicitly implements `decisions/log.md`, 2026-09-27: recognized package payments wait for Joshua to click \"Grant N passes\" on teacher Home. The live response and live ledger match that implementation. There is no configuration flag to enable automatic package grants in this version. Joshua's current request asks to reconsider this deliberate prior policy.\n@@\n-## Recommended resolution\n+## Reconcile the manual grant before enabling automatic grants\n@@\n-First inspect the delivery of Celia's exact event in Stripe and the function's corresponding response/error, then read its commerce rows using an authorized service-role or admin diagnostic endpoint. Confirm which deployed function version ran. The local logic should have credited this exact payment, so changing email matching or offer mapping would be speculative.\n+The manual pass's notes now contain the exact event, intent, charge, ledger ID, amount, payment date, and an explicit statement that this pass fulfills the purchase. This was written through `booking-pass-admin` action `update`, then independently reread. Five total, zero used, and active status remained unchanged.\n@@\n-Protect the manual restoration from duplicate credits before retries: reconcile the original event to the already granted pass and record it as fulfilled through a transaction that does not grant more sessions. **Do not replay Celia's event or run the normal credit RPC now**, because the manual pass has no Stripe event-id marker.\n+The newer migration `20260927090000_stripe_payments_ledger.sql` provides `admin_dismiss_stripe_payment(_payment_id uuid)`. It changes `awaiting` to `dismissed` without adding credits and preserves a matched student. It can safely remove this already handled purchase from the grant queue, although `dismissed` is not a formal `granted` fulfillment record. This investigation did not invoke it. **Do not call `admin_grant_stripe_payment` now**: it would add another five sessions. Its current implementation protects `granted` rows but does not reject `dismissed` rows, so dismissal alone must not become the only automatic-grant guard.\n@@\n-Add an admin payment diagnostic/reconciliation surface, durable paid-payment receipt records keyed by checkout session/payment intent, and a retry worker that reconciles unfulfilled receipts. Handle delayed payment success, unknown offers, and unmatched students explicitly. Surface actionable failures to Joshua without sending student messages automatically.\n+Minimal policy change: preserve the existing Stripe ledger and teacher review for unmatched or unknown purchases; after successful live-mode, paid-status, known-package, price/currency, and exact account matching checks, invoke a service-role-only transactional grant function keyed by the existing Stripe payment row. It must lock the payment row, only accept `awaiting`, grant exactly once, and preserve `dismissed`/`granted` decisions. Use this shared transaction for both automatic and teacher-confirmed grants, with a separate admin-authorized reconciliation operation that can mark an existing manual pass as fulfilling a payment without changing its balance.
